# Daemon Slayer per-champion meta-valuation sweep - tracker

Standing operator directive (2026-07-17): sweep ALL 173 champions ALPHABETICALLY from Aatrox,
fine-detail on the kit damage axis + anything else per champ, and report completed + remaining each session.
Methodology: memory feedback_ds_sweep_meta_valuation_research. Read-only GAP-or-REFUTE pass FIRST per champ;
a REFUTE is a valid outcome; NO engine change in the research pass (GAP fixes are operator-gated Tier-2 builds).
Prior cherry-picked ADCs stay completed. Each verdict has its own project_ds_sweep_<champ>_* memory.

Legend: [ ] PENDING  -  [GAP RM-NN] resolved gap (spec RM-NN)  -  [REFUTE] resolved no-change  -  [FENCED] data-refuted do-not-re-pitch.

## Summary

- Resolved: 173 / 173  (GAP 135, REFUTE 35, FENCED/data-refuted 3)
- Remaining: 0  -  **ROSTER CLOSED 2026-07-18 (batch32).** The operator-directed
  all-173 alphabetical sweep that began at Aatrox is COMPLETE. Do NOT re-open it
  or re-scan for uncovered champions; further growth needs a schema lift, not
  another roster pass.
- **COUNT INTEGRITY (fixed 2026-07-18, batch18).** The header count above and the
  `## Full roster` checkboxes are two independent records and they had silently

  incremented this count, but never flipped the ten roster checkboxes
  (Morgana / Naafiri / Nami / Nasus / Nautilus / Neeko / Nidalee / Nilah / Nocturne /
  Nunu & Willump). Backfilled in batch18; roster and header now agree exactly
  (70 + 31 + 3 = 104 resolved, 69 pending, 173 total; batch19 then took it to
  74 + 32 + 3 = 109 resolved, 64 pending). **Verify the ROSTER, not this line, when
  reporting progress - and re-run the count after every batch.** One-liner:
  `grep -c '^- \[ \]' docs/DS_SWEEP_TRACKER.md` must equal Remaining.
  **USE THE GREP ONE-LINER. Do NOT verify by string-splitting this file on the
  roster heading** - any prose that mentions the heading (including this note) adds
  another occurrence, so a split grabs the wrong fragment and silently reports ZERO
  resolved. Batch19 hit that, batch20 hit it AGAIN after "fixing" it by writing a
  more specific split literal into this very note, which immediately made that
  literal ambiguous too. The counting recipe that cannot break:
  `grep -c '^- \[GAP' `, `grep -c '^- \[REFUTE\]' `, `grep -c '^- \[FENCED' `,
  `grep -c '^- \[ \] ' ` - the first three must sum to Resolved and the fourth must
  equal Remaining.

  find-and-replace across them silently no-ops. Edit each line individually and
  ASSERT the replacement count is 1. Both traps were caught only because the counts
  were re-verified AFTER writing rather than inferred from the write succeeding.

  read that occurrence as a live pointer. Verify the true max across BOTH the working
  tree and every live lane branch before taking an id:
  `git fetch origin && git show origin/lane/research:ROADMAP.md | grep -ohE "RM-[0-9]+"`
  alongside the working-tree grep on the line below.
- **RM-86 L1 SHIPPED 2026-07-18 (LEDGER 940, ENGINE 1.218.0)** - the sweep's first
  engine change. `agents/daemon_slayer/kit_conversion.py` + a default-OFF
  `kit_conversion_strength` lever on carry / assassin / mage / tank. Two spec
  corrections landed with it (spec section 10), and BOTH matter for future verdicts:
  (1) the conversion vector CANNOT be derived from `damage_blocks` - no
  attack-speed / crit / on-hit / DoT key exists in any of the 1709 blocks across
  171 champions, and the loader drops `effects_descriptions`, so it is a
  prose-seeded curated registry;
  (2) a monotone-lowering sort-key gate can only push bad items DOWN, never push a
  good item UP past untouched neighbours - so any future verdict whose remedy is
  "champion X's real item must RISE" is L2 objective-coverage work, NOT an L1 case.
  Olaf Stridebreaker and Pantheon Black Cleaver / Heartsteel were reclassified that
  way on measurement. Naafiri, Orianna and the Poppy control were REACHED.
  Correction for anyone quoting batch18: Olaf's Stridebreaker baseline is **#34**,
  not #33; Olaf W attack speed is 40/50/60/70/80 pct, not 50-90; and Rek'Sai's
  "3 autos per Fury cycle" is NOT in 16.14.1 prose - Q reads "Her next basic
  attack", singular.

- **BATCH32 HEADLINE - the two new shapes are mirror images across two scorers.**
  RM-96 (Zilean) is `ds.hps` pricing heal/shield-CONDITIONAL passives at FULL value
  on a champion who cannot trigger them - an OVER-valuation. RM-97 (Zyra) is
  `ds.ability` pricing a persistent auto-attacking pet at ZERO because a
  `damage_block` is a per-cast instantaneous magnitude with no slot for it - an
  UNDER-valuation. Both are the same root category as RM-92 (the objective cannot
  represent the thing the item or kit actually does), which now spans four scorers.
- **RM-96 (NEW SHAPE) - `ds.hps` prices heal/shield-CONDITIONAL item passives at
  full value regardless of whether the champion can trigger them.** The canonical
  instance is Zilean, whose live enchanter pool is EXACTLY INVERTED at the #4/#5
  boundary: ranks #1-#4 (Echoes of Helia, Ardent Censer, Staff of Flowing Water,
  Knight's Vow) have ZERO real pick rate on two independent sources, and ranks
  #5-#8 (Locket, Imperial Mandate, Redemption, Mikael's) are every real item that
  exists in the pool. Mechanism, measured by regex over `items.json`: Echoes,
  Ardent, Staff and Moonstone ALL gate their unique passive on "Healing or
  Shielding an ally". `champion_abilities.json` (is_current=True, so NOT an RM-81
  or RM-95 artifact) shows Zilean has ZERO shield blocks anywhere and exactly ONE
  heal - R Chronoshift, which fires only on FATAL damage at a 120/90/60s cooldown.
  Three items whose passive he structurally cannot trigger rank #1/#2/#3.
  **This is the exact mirror of RM-92:** RM-92 is an item doing something real the
  objective cannot see; RM-96 is an item doing NOTHING the objective credits anyway.
  Note it is NOT fixed by the champion-invariance work - the invariance control
  (Zilean / Soraka / Yuumi / Janna / Lulu return byte-identical #1-#8) shows the
  order is closed-pool-invariant, so per `project_ds_hps_champion_invariant` the
  adjudication target is the ROUTE and the CONDITIONALITY, not the order.
- **RM-97 (NEW SHAPE) - persistent pet / summon damage is structurally unmodelled;
  a `damage_block` has no slot for it.** The canonical instance is Zyra, whose
  plants are 55-65 percent of her damage (73 percent while R enrages them) and
  which the ability scorer prices at exactly ZERO. This is a SCHEMA hole, not a
  missing formula: a `damage_block` is a per-cast instantaneous magnitude, and a
  persistent auto-attacking entity has no per-cast magnitude to record. Confirmed
  to GENERALIZE - the same hole applies to Heimerdinger (turrets), Ivern (Daisy)
  and Yorick (Maiden + ghouls), so this is a four-champion class, not a Zyra
  accident. Distinguish it from RM-95: the ability data is PRESENT and CURRENT for
  all four; the schema simply cannot express what the kit does. **Consequence for
  the RM-86 L2 plan: a pet-damage term is a numerator the scorer does not have, so
  no monotone-lowering L1 gate can reach it** - same structural verdict already
  recorded for RM-91 and RM-92.
  **PROBED ON DISK 2026-07-28 (R225) - the spec HOLDS, with one correction and one
  measured nuance.** Correction: the "prices them at exactly ZERO" phrasing is right
  for the numerator but wrong as a blanket claim - two NON-damage axes DO credit the
  class today, `objdamage.py:79-83` SUMMON_DPS 0.55 and `zonecontrol.py:73-77`
  SUMMON 0.60, both of which name Zyra plants / Heimerdinger turrets / Yorick ghouls
  + Maiden / Malzahar voidlings / Annie Tibbers / Elise spiders / Ivern Daisy
  explicitly. The hole is the DAMAGE numerator only. Nuance: the class splits in two.
  Pet-bearing forms with ZERO `attribute_kind == "damage"` blocks (measured, 7 forms):
  Zyra P Garden of Thorns, Zyra W Rampant Growth, Heimerdinger Q both forms
  (H-28G Evolution Turret + H-28Q Apex Turret), Ivern R Daisy!, Yorick P Shepherd of
  Souls, and Yorick R Eulogy of the Isles whose single block is `("Mist Walkers",
  "other")` - a COUNT, correctly excluded by the `attribute_kind` filter at
  `abilities.py:274` / `ability_dps.py:459`. Against that, three summon forms DO carry
  exactly one damage block and are credited: Malzahar W Void Swarm, Annie R Summon:
  Tibbers (the summon burst only), Elise W Volatile Spiderling (a one-shot explosion).
  So the accurate statement is "no PERSISTENT-ENTITY uptime model", NOT "no summon
  ever scores". Engine measurement, Zyra level 11 / SR / armor 30 / MR 30 / no items:
  per_spell W dps 0.0, Q 9.147609147609147, E 1.2968849332485697, R 1.0865999671969822,
  total 11.5310940480547; the P row is ABSENT rather than zero because `SPELL_KEYS`
  (`ability_dps.py:209`) is `("Q","W","E","R")`. Pinned by
  `agents/daemon_slayer/tests/test_pet_summon_damage_uncredited_rm97.py` (12 tests /
  27 subtests). Still SPEC-ONLY - no engine math shipped; a fix remains a schema lift.
- **RM-98 (ALLOCATED 2026-07-19, NOT a champion GAP) - the AD-axis ability term
  sums two rates that are on DIFFERENT TIME BASES.** Surfaced by the RM-39/RM-43
  L2 build (ENGINE 1.223.0), not by the alphabetical sweep, so it carries no
  roster checkbox and leaves the Summary counts untouched. With
  `apply_ad_axis_ability_damage` ON, `ds.hybrid` adds a GAME-AVERAGE ability rate
  to a COMBAT-WINDOW auto rate. `casts_per_sec` comes from
  `data/daemon_slayer/spell_cast_rates.json`, computed as
  `spell[1-4]_casts / game_duration_s` over 2851 matches, so it includes laning,
  recalls and death timers: Renekton W reads **0.0184/s** (about one cast per
  minute) against the rotation's modelled `w: 1.5` casts in a 3-second window =
  **0.5/s**, a **27x** discrepancy. The effect is a systematic UNDER-pricing of
  the ability term relative to the auto term it is added to, which distorts
  ability-item versus auto-item valuation.
  **ADJUDICATED 2026-07-19 - `docs/specs/SPEC_rm98_cast_rate_time_base.md`.**
  Verdict: the whole-game basis is **neither deliberate nor accidental, it is a
  documented-but-unadjudicated inheritance**. The one recorded deliberate choice
  was measured vs `1/cooldown` (`docs/history_notes.md:12534`); the DENOMINATOR
  was never in the option set, and the shape was inherited by explicit admission
  (`scripts/build_spell_cast_rates.py:9-11`) from an s145 ad-hoc query whose own
  consumer already fed a "DPS-time proc rate" (`ult_rates.py:5-7`).
  **The defence in the RM-98 filing is RETRACTED:** haste-inclusiveness follows
  from EMPIRICISM, not from the denominator, and RM-39 did not rely on the
  whole-game basis - it condemned it and prescribed replacing it
  (`SPEC_rm39_rm43_ability_haste.md:187-192`, `:174-176`, `:318-320`).
  Four corrections to the filing above: (1) the defect ALREADY SHIPS DEFAULT-ON
  in `ds.onhit` (`onhit_dps.py:143`), `ds.dps` (`dps.py:1023`) and `ds.hps`
  (`hps.py:679`), so gating a default-OFF flag on it is incoherent; (2) the term
  is NOT inert - 29.2% cohort mean of the AD-axis damage term, 65.9% Riven, and
  it re-orders; (3) 27x overstates - Q 7.83x / W 27.12x / E 6.44x, and the
  0.5/s reference comes from a `scenarios.json` field `_rotation_attack_dps`
  never reads (`dps.py:549-566`); (4) under-scoped - it misses
  `ability_dps.py:1252`, where 6 champions sum a `1/cooldown` combat basis and
  the whole-game measured basis in ONE total.
  **Both fixes RM-39 named are INFEASIBLE (sized this session):** casts-per-
  second-alive lifts only **1.25x** against a 27x gap (`time_spent_dead` median
  0.204), and an in-combat rate is not derivable because `timeline_frames`
  cadence is **60s** (measured 60016-60021 ms) versus a 3-10s combat window.
  **Recommendation: do NOT chase a denominator replacement.** Demote the
  measured rate from a DPS multiplier to a **cast-propensity prior**
  (`SPEC_rm39_rm43_ability_haste.md:198-202`), which needs correct relative
  ordering, not a correct denominator.
  - **Ziggs** (mage) - REFUTE. His ranked head is byte-identical to Xerath's and
    Vex's for all ten top items (exact-prefix agreement 10, top-20 set identical),
    so it carries ZERO champion-specific signal and every divergence from his real
    Luden's-first build is an already-specced cohort template (RM-86 / RM-90 /
    RM-94), not a Ziggs finding. His one defining trait IS modelled and modelled
    well: `objdamage.py` registers ('Ziggs','P','STRUCTURE_BONUS','STRUCTURE',
    magnitude=0.92) - the highest structure magnitude on the roster (next is Yunara
    0.68) - in an axis that is architecturally default-inert for all 173, the same
    shape as the boot-utility scorer already adjudicated not-a-gap. Cohort check
    per RM-90 (which warns that REFUTEs skipping the shipped build order were judged
    TOO LENIENT): 45 mage-routed champions collapse to 6 distinct shipped builds,
    44/45 open Liandry's, and Ziggs shares his EXACT 6-item shipped build with 16
    other champions - so the template is confirmed at the ARTIFACT layer too, and
    it is a cohort defect already on the books rather than his.
  - **Zilean** (enchanter) - GAP RM-96, the canonical instance. See the spec above.
    Route confirmed correct via `core/archetype_picks.py:83`
    `_TAG_TO_ARCHETYPE['support']='enchanter'`; the axis correction cannot fire
    because `_ARCHETYPE_AXIS` already maps enchanter->'ap' and he is 0.836 magical,
    so this is an AP-vs-AP blind spot rather than a routing error.
  - **Zoe** (mage) - GAP RM-40, and a CLEANER canonical instance than the previous
    Orianna: BOTH engine head items are absent from her real build while her
    ~80%-presence Luden's Echo ranks #13. The mechanism is now PROVEN rather than
    inferred - exact float ties across her ranked rows show `ds.ability` prices
    every per-cast proc passive at exactly zero. No new spec number; she is a
    textbook burst-mage instance of the existing family.
  - **Zyra** (mage) - GAP RM-97, the canonical instance. See the spec above.
- **BATCH29/30/31 HEADLINE - the defect is ARCHETYPE INVARIANCE, measured twice.**
  This batch stopped producing per-champion findings and started producing one
  systemic measurement, from two independent directions:
  (1) **The mage head is EXACTLY invariant across 7 mages spanning two batches**
  (Twisted Fate / Veigar / Vel'Koz / Vex / Viktor / Vladimir / Xerath):
  Liandry's Torment **#1 for all seven**, Blackfire Torch **#2 for all seven**,
  Mejai's **#6 for all seven**, Rabadon's and Void Staff merely swapping #3/#4,
  and Luden's Echo **#13 for six of seven**. Only ONE of the seven has its real
  first legendary at the top (Viktor, Blackfire 76.78% -> #2).
  (2) **BotRK ranks #1 on 11 of 11 AD-routed champions** probed this batch -
  every bruiser and every carry, no exceptions.
  **This mechanically explains a standing sweep observation.** The precompute
  histogram has recorded since batch15 that "Luden's Echo appears ZERO times" as
  a first legendary across all 173 champions, treated until now as a curiosity.
  The cause is measured: Luden's ranks #13 invariantly, so it can never surface
  as a first item - while being the real first legendary for Xerath (87.10%),
  Vex (88.44%) and Vel'Koz (63.67%). The zero is a consequence of the invariant
  head, not a data quirk.
  **Framing that keeps this honest: the BotRK lead is not uniformly WRONG, it is
  uniformly UNCONDITIONAL.** Warwick is the counter-example - BotRK genuinely is
  his #1 real item at 76.95% presence, and Yone's at 91.8%, so the engine is
  right for them. Viego (batch26) proves it can be wrong, since his Q already
  applies the same %HP on-hit and the real item is dead at 4.12% presence and a
  LOSING 46.15% WR. The defect is not the lead; it is that nothing distinguishes
  the two cases. This is RM-90's mega-templates observed at the RANKING layer and
  is the cleanest quantification of RM-86 so far, because the invariance is
  EXACT rather than approximate.
- **RM-95 (NEW SHAPE) - ability-data COVERAGE and the staleness checker that
  hides it. This is a DIAGNOSTIC defect, not a scoring defect - scope it that way.**
  Surfaced by `fell_back=True` on Wukong and Zaahen, the first two occurrences in
  29 probed champions. `core/daemon_slayer_client.py:1423` computes
  `"fell_back": fell_back or not champion_has_ability_data(champion)`.
  A full-roster scan for "no ability data yet is_current=True" returns FIVE
  champions - Locke, Nunu & Willump, Renata Glasc, Wukong, Zaahen - splitting in two:
  **(A) ALIAS MISSES (3), where the data EXISTS and is simply not found.**
  `champion_abilities.json` is keyed by DDragon id. Punctuation and whitespace
  normalization works exactly as its docstring claims (verified: Lee Sin/LeeSin and
  Vel'Koz/Velkoz both resolve), but it cannot handle a SEMANTIC rename:
  Wukong -> `MonkeyKing`, Nunu & Willump -> `Nunu`, Renata Glasc -> `Renata`.
  Only these three exist on the roster; a 3-entry alias map fixes it.
  **(B) GENUINELY ABSENT (2): Locke and Zaahen.** `champion_abilities.json` holds
  171 champions while `/health` and `champions.json` both report 173.
  **(C) The real defect is that the staleness checker certifies missing data as
  clean.** `champion_ability_data_is_current()` returns True for all five, because
  data that is absent cannot be DRIFTED. Vacuous truth. The RM-81 program
  (`tools/ds_wiki_staleness_check.py`, `ability_staleness.json`) therefore
  UNDER-REPORTS by exactly the set it should flag hardest. It needs a third state,
  "no data", distinct from "not drifted". A verification tool that reports clean on
  absent data is worse than a scorer gap because it hides the problem.
  **SCOPE CORRECTION made before this reached the tracker:** the first write-up
  claimed these champions' RANKINGS were degraded and floated that the batch17
  "Nunu [GAP route]" verdict was mis-attributed. Both were wrong and are retracted.
  Ranking by display name vs DDragon id is BYTE-IDENTICAL for all three alias
  cases (only the `fell_back` flag differs), which is consistent with the closed
  Vayne finding that `ds.dps`/`ds.ehp`/`ds.hps` never import `abilities` at all;
  and the batch17 Nunu analysis used the id form, so that verdict stands.
  `fell_back` is additionally BRANCH-dependent - the tank / enchanter branches
  hardcode it False (lines 1464, 1511, 1551) - so it cannot detect this in general
  and surfaced Wukong only by luck.
- **ZAAHEN - the most complete champion-representation failure found in the sweep,
  and the reason RM-95 is worth a fix.** Two defects COMPOUND on one champion, both
  verified in repo data. (1) `champions.json` gives him `attackdamageperlevel = 0`.
  That is CORRECT upstream data, not corruption: Zaahen (172nd champion, released
  2025-11-19 patch 25.23) routes all AD growth through his passive Cultivation of
  War - 12 Determination stacks, each ~1.5-2.95% AD by level, DOUBLING at max stacks
  to roughly 36-70.87% AD by level. (2) His ability data is absent entirely, so that
  passive is invisible. **The engine therefore models him as 63 AD that never grows,
  with no abilities** - at L16 it sees 63 AD where Yasuo (AD/lvl 3.0) has 105. Both
  halves of his damage model are missing at once and neither can cover for the other.
  Observable in the output: his real first item is Trinity Force at 70.46% top /
  81.17% jungle and it ranks **#6**, behind BotRK #1, Heartsteel #2, Stormrazor #3,
  Kraken #4 and **Liandry's Torment #5 - an AP item on a champion with ZERO AP
  ratios anywhere in his kit** (physical 22,668 / true 1,636 / magic 1,278). Real
  2nd/3rd Stridebreaker 46.37% and Death's Dance 35.42% sit at #45 and #33.
  Generalisable hazard: `attackdamageperlevel = 0` will mis-price ANY champion who
  routes growth through a passive; worth a targeted roster scan.
- **BATCH29/30/31 PER-CHAMPION EVIDENCE** (live 1.219.0 / 16.14.1, top=200, corrected
  recipe; EHP-routed champions additionally swept on `enemy_ad_share`).
  Format: real presence -> engine rank.
  - **Yuumi** (enchanter, pool 10) - **NOW THE MOST COMPLETE RM-92 INSTANCE, past
    Soraka.** Dream Maker **91.4% -> NOT A CANDIDATE** (RM-93); Moonstone Renewer
    64.9% -> **#10 of 10, dead last**; Mikael's Blessing 59.8% -> #8; Ardent Censer
    51.0% -> #2. Her Moonstone rank is IDENTICAL to Soraka's because `ds.hps` is
    champion-invariant, so it is the same #10 for every enchanter. There is no
    self-output item anywhere in her build: her top four purchases are ally-buff,
    ally-heal, ally-cleanse and ally-damage-redirection, and her kit passes E's
    shield and speed to the ally instead of herself. She is also a strict TRAP-D -
    her only played role loses (48.31-49.38% vs a 51.65% bracket average).
    **Engine credit where due: the shipped artifact correctly gives her NO boots**
    (she is Attached and buys none in ~98% of games). She and Cassiopeia are the
    only two no-boot champions shipped, though this falls out of the enchanter pool
    excluding boots rather than from a Yuumi-specific rule.
  - **Xayah** (carry) - cleanest PAIRED miss in the sweep: engine ranks **Runaan's
    Hurricane #2** while it is absent from her core, absent from every slot table and
    absent from a 20-deep popularity list, with a mechanical cause (Runaan's bolts do
    not generate feathers, and her damage is feather-gated) - and simultaneously her
    **highest-presence legendary, Navori Flickerblade at 87.0%, ranks #30**. Wrong
    item promoted and right item buried on one champion. Essence Reaver 63.39% -> #17,
    IE 71.78% -> #6. ABILITY DATA DRIFTED.
  - **Xerath** (mage) - **new canonical RM-93 instance, stronger than Vel'Koz.**
    Support builds **Zaz'Zak's Realmspike at 94.28% presence** (vs Vel'Koz 86.82%)
    and it is a candidate on ZERO routes; its 49.63% WR sits BELOW his support
    average, independently confirming it is bought for support-quest income and
    vision rather than output. Mid: Luden's 87.10% -> #13 while engine gives Void
    Staff #3 (real 11.36%) and Rabadon's #4 (real 35.57%).
  - **Yasuo** / **Yone** - GAP RM-86, and the batch's best-controlled measurement.
    Their passive DOUBLES total crit chance, crits deal 180% AD (207% with IE)
    instead of 200%, and excess crit above 100% converts at 0.5 AD per 1%. Measured
    consequence: expected crit items in the first five legendaries is **2.18-2.42
    for Yasuo/Yone against 3.97-4.04 for Caitlyn and Jinx** on the same patch and
    the same method - almost exactly the halving a 2x multiplier predicts. A scorer
    pricing crit linearly over-ranks crit items for them ~2x and simultaneously
    UNDER-ranks the 3rd/4th crit item, whose crit is really 25 bonus AD.
    They also buy NONE of the zero-AD crit items (RFC 0.00%, Runaan's 0.00%,
    Phantom Dancer 2.0-2.8%) because once crit caps in two items the marginal crit
    item is valued as an AD stick. **Yasuo is a confirmed TRAP-D**: mid 63.1% at
    49.84% versus bottom 10.7% at 52.43%, a +2.6pp swing (upper bound - an off-role
    that small self-selects for mains). Yone is NOT a trap-D and BotRK #1 is
    genuinely correct for him at 91.8% presence.
  - **SHIELDBOW CONTROL - a natural experiment, and it REFINED my hypothesis rather
    than confirming it.** Immortal Shieldbow and Hexoptics C44 carry byte-identical
    statlines in the engine's own data (both 55 AD / 25% crit, same gold), yet
    Yasuo and Yone take Shieldbow 79-93% and Hexoptics 0.00-0.03%, isolating the
    Lifeline shield as the entire purchase driver. Predicted: the scorer is blind to
    the difference and ranks them adjacently. **Measured: it is NOT blind** -
    Shieldbow #19/#20/#16 versus Hexoptics #30/#31/#26 for Yasuo/Yone/Xin Zhao, so
    the engine separates them by 10-11 places in the CORRECT direction. The real
    defect is magnitude, not blindness: reality is total substitution while the
    engine puts Yasuo's ~87%-presence second core at #19 of 143.
  - **Zeri** (carry) - GAP RM-86, third distinct BotRK kit-blindness instance.
    Her right-click is a charged shot that is MAGIC, scales with AP and target max
    health, CANNOT crit, and **does not apply or trigger on-hit effects**; her Q is
    a spell that applies on-hit to the first enemy only, can crit, and converts
    attack speed past a 1.5-casts/sec cap into AD at 60%. The engine still leads her
    with **BotRK #1** - an on-hit %current-HP item on a champion whose basic attack
    does not apply on-hit. IE 71.22% (her real #1) -> #13; Stormrazor 67.24% -> #4.
    Her anomalous top-6 (Dusk and Dawn #2, Liandry's #3) traces to base stats: AD/lvl
    2.0 and AS/lvl 2 against Xayah's 3.5 and 3.9, so a pure auto-attack sim converts
    AD/crit poorly for her. ABILITY DATA DRIFTED.
  - **Zac** (tank) - GAP RM-92, heal-AMPLIFICATION unpriced. **Spirit Visage at
    62.70% presence heals for ZERO by itself**; its entire marginal value on Zac is
    multiplying a kit heal (passive goo chunks heal 4-8.47% max health each, plus
    Cell Division's revive). A scorer pricing heal/shield power from an item's own
    healing output values it at ~0. Engine: Spirit Visage #8, Sunfire Aegis 80.53%
    -> #11, while Randuin's #1 (real 15.77%) and Warmog's #2 and Heartsteel #5 are
    all low-presence - the Ornn/Rammus RM-87 shape recurring. His kit has AP ratios
    on every ability (Q 60%, E 80%, R 100% AP) yet the meta buys ZERO AP, and bonus
    HP barely feeds his damage (only Q, at 6% of bonus health), so HP is EHP not
    damage for him. ABILITY DATA DRIFTED.
  - **Vladimir** (mage) - GAP RM-92 + a clean RM-91 instance. Crimson Pact makes
    3.333% bonus health into AP and 160% AP into bonus health, so HP and AP are
    literally the SAME STAT on him. His real #1, Hextech Rocketbelt at 64%, ranks
    **#26** and is bought for the 275-unit dash - his only mobility besides a
    self-damaging pool. The tell is an inversion: Rocketbelt is his highest-presence
    item AND his lowest win rate (50.15%), while Rabadon's at lower presence wins
    58.34%. Riftmaker 41.83% -> #10, Stormsurge 43.43% -> #7.

    Transcendence has no crit requirement - he buys attack speed and cooldown
    throughput. Dusk and Dawn 72.13% -> #5. SOFT trap-D (top 74.7% is the worse role
    on both sources but only sub-50% on one, so flagged soft not confirmed).

    summed Lethal Tempo share is 60.3%. ABILITY DATA DRIFTED.
  - **Warwick** (bruiser) - GAP RM-92, but **the engine's #1 is CORRECT**: BotRK is

    scorers: his damage and healing are the SAME number (R heals 100% of
    post-mitigation damage, passive heals 100%/250% at low HP), so the terms are

  - **Wukong** (bruiser) - GAP RM-86. Trinity Force 91.26% (real #1) -> #2 and

  - **Yorick** (bruiser) - GAP RM-92. Trinity Force 63.3% -> #2 correct; Spear of

- **BATCH26/27/28 METHOD CORRECTIONS - the standing probe recipe is DRIFTED.**
  (1) `rank_for_primary_archetype` returns its rows under the key **`ranked`**,
  NOT `rows` / `results`. A recipe-faithful probe returns pool=0 for every
  champion and looks exactly like a total engine failure. (2) There is **no
  `champion_has_ability_data` key in the response at all**; the standing recipe
  says to print it. Currency comes from the separate
  `core.daemon_slayer_client.champion_ability_data_is_current(champ)`.
  (3) The tanky-vs-squishy profile pair varies only DPS-side inputs, which
  `ds.ehp` ignores - so a tank returning an IDENTICAL order across both profiles
  is CORRECT, not target-blindness. To probe a tank, vary `enemy_ad_share` /
  `enemy_ap_share` instead (done here: the EHP scorer flips armor-vs-MR items
  correctly, so it is comp-responsive).
  (4) Item files have three different shapes and none of them is guessable:
  `items.json` is DDragon-shaped (`j['data']`, stats keys like
  `FlatPhysicalDamageMod`), `items_meraki.json` carries no `stats` block at all,
  and `build_orders_sr.json` nests under `build_orders` with per-champion
  `{ad_heavy, ap_heavy, balanced}` id lists. Read the shape before indexing it.
- **RM-93 (NEW SHAPE) - support-quest item CANDIDACY is inconsistent across pools.**
  The World Atlas 386x-387x line splits three ways and the split is not principled:
  Dream Maker 3870 / Celestial Opposition 3869 / Solstice Sleigh 3876 ARE candidates
  on mage + tank (always as a contiguous bottom block - Vel'Koz mage #116-#118,
  Thresh tank #61-#63) but are absent from the enchanter pool; Zaz'Zak's Realmspike
  3871 and Bloodsong 3877 are candidates on ZERO routes despite both carrying full
  modelled damage formulas with an explicit guard suite
  (`agents/daemon_slayer/tests/test_effects_expansion.py:7197`, AP+HP scaling and
  AoE-scales-with-targets). So the batch21 Seraphine note needs refining: Dream
  Maker is excluded from `ds.hps` SPECIFICALLY, not globally. The enchanter pool is
  10 items and excludes all five, i.e. the one archetype that actually BUYS these
  items is the one route where none of them are candidates. Canonical instance:
  **Vel'Koz's real first item is Zaz'Zak's Realmspike at 86.82% presence and it is
  a candidate nowhere.** This is filter-list work, not L2 objective work.
- **RM-94 (NEW SHAPE) - snowball-conditional stacks are pinned at FULL value.**
  `agents/daemon_slayer/_effects_data.py:3257` pins Mejai's Soulstealer at
  `bonus_ap_stacked=125.0` (25 stacks x 5 AP) under an explicit "same sustained-peak
  convention as Black Cleaver at full stacks / Riftmaker at full ramp" comment. The
  convention is defensible for Black Cleaver (5 stacks is reached inside a single
  fight - Urgot applies all 5 in under two seconds at his fixed 3.0 attack speed)
  but NOT for Mejai's, where 25 stacks is a GAME-STATE that requires already having
  won, and which is the cause of the item's win rate rather than its effect.
  Measured consequence: **Mejai's ranks #6 for all five mages probed this batch**
  (Twisted Fate / Veigar / Vel'Koz / Vex / Viktor - byte-identical position) against
  real presence of 2.02% (Veigar mid) to 5.65% (Vex). The fix is to separate
  "reachable within a fight" from "requires already being ahead", not to delete the
  pin. Note this is NOT the win-rate-honeypot failure the research line predicted:
  DS default ranking is pure simulation, so the engine reaches the same wrong answer
  by a completely different route.
- **BATCH26/27/28 HEADLINE - RM-92 now has a MECHANICALLY DETECTABLE trigger: the
  zero-AD Zeal line.** Verified on both lines (research claim, then independently
  confirmed against `data/daemon_slayer/16.14.1/items.json`). The 2026 Zeal rework
  left four items with NO `FlatPhysicalDamageMod` at all:
  Fiendhunter Bolts 2512, Phantom Dancer 3046, Rapid Firecannon 3094,
  Navori Flickerblade 6675 - each crit .25 / AS / MS and ZERO AD.
  Controls behave correctly: Infinity Edge 3031 (AD 75) ranks #3-#5, and
  Statikk Shiv 3087 is AD 45 + AP 45 hybrid, which confirms the batch24 Teemo note.
  Each zero-AD item ranks DEEP on exactly the champion whose real build is built
  around it, and each is bought for a NON-OUTPUT property:
    Twitch       Fiendhunter Bolts  69.70% presence, real 2nd core -> **#76 of 111**
                 (30 ULTIMATE ability haste = Spray-and-Pray uptime; corroborated by
                 the Sorcery secondary running Axiom Arcanist at 60.3%, also ult-facing)
    TwistedFate  Rapid Firecannon   67.30% presence, real 3rd core -> **#121 of 144**
                 (Sharpshooter +35% energized range, cap +150, pushes the Pick a Card
                 auto to ~675 range - it is a GOLD CARD STUN DELIVERY item, not damage)
    Tristana     Navori Flickerblade 79.28% presence, real co-core -> **#34 of 111**
                 (basic-ability CDR incl. W Rocket Jump, her only mobility/escape)
    Tryndamere   Phantom Dancer     35.00% presence               -> **#54 of 143**
                 (MS + self-shield; kiting and survival)
  This is not a new shape - it is RM-92 with an enumerable instance class sharing ONE
  stat signature. That matters because the trigger is a detectable statline rather
  than a per-champion judgement call, which makes it the cheapest RM-92 lead so far.
- **BATCH26/27/28 PER-CHAMPION EVIDENCE** (probe: live 1.219.0 / 16.14.1, top=200,

  all 14 probed returned ok=True / fell_back=False). Format: real presence -> engine rank.

    His own damage is ~1/10th of a carry's, so the unpriced axis is ally-facing value,

  - **Udyr** (bruiser). Spear of Shojin 60.52% -> #51; Sundered Sky #11; Death's Dance

  - **Vayne** (carry) - BACKFILLED, NOT RE-PROBED. Closed 2026-07-18 in

  - **Vi** (bruiser). Genuinely BIMODAL - bruiser line ~62% (Sundered Sky 61.95% ->

    Her W (not Q) carries the 20% armor reduction, which stacks multiplicatively with
    Black Cleaver to ~44% and is why LDR sits at only 14.39% despite a 56% WR.
    A single "core build" for Vi is a 41-62% plurality at best.
  - **Viego** (bruiser). BEST RM-86 INSTANCE IN THE SWEEP TO DATE, and it is verifiable
    from repo data alone: the engine ranks **BotRK #1**, but the ITEM is dead in the
    real meta at 4.12% presence and a LOSING 46.15% WR - because his Q is literally
    named Blade of the Ruined King and already applies the same effect. Confirmed in
    `data/daemon_slayer/16.14.1/champion_abilities.json`: Viego Q damage_blocks carry
    `target_current_hp_pct [2,3,4,5,6]`. Meanwhile his highest-presence real item,
    The Collector at 84.77%, ranks **#27**; Kraken Slayer 69.3% -> #4;
    Immortal Shieldbow 56.56% -> #15. This is kit-blindness in REVERSE: the scorer
    prices an item's %HP on-hit without checking whether the kit already supplies it,
    so a duplicated effect is double-counted. It is also a concrete case where the
    global BotRK over-lead (64 of 173 first legendaries) is provably wrong.
  - **Viktor** (mage, cohort 17). Blackfire Torch 76.78% (real #1, and first in all six
    top core sets) -> engine #2, with Liandry's #1 at only 18.15% real presence.
    Rabadon's engine #3 but real 18.29% and a 4th/5th-slot luxury, NOT a core.
    Hextech Rocketbelt 36.30% -> #28 is his only mobility in a kit with zero dashes
    (RM-92, same shape as Sylas). Kit verified NOT unrecognizably reworked. His
    keystone is Deathfire Touch at 90.75% row-summed - one of the two NEW S2 Sorcery

- **RM-92 NOTE (NEW SHAPE) - NON-OUTPUT item value is unpriced.** Every scorer's
  objective is the champion's OWN damage or healing throughput, so an item bought
  for a property that is not self-output scores near zero no matter how universally
  it is built. Six of these ten carry it, and the properties are distinct enough to
  be worth listing because each is a separate missing term: **AoE conversion**
  (Soraka's Moonstone), **mobility** (Sylas's Hextech Rocketbelt), **CC uptime**
  (Swain's Rylai's), **team-wide damage amplification** (Sona's Bloodsong),
  **ally-facing stats** (Taric's Locket / Knight's Vow / Zeke's), and **ability
  haste as an enabler** (Smolder's Black Cleaver + Spear of Shojin).
  **CANONICAL INSTANCE = Soraka, and it is the most extreme single measurement in
  the sweep to date: her 90.28%-presence near-mandatory first legendary, Moonstone
  Renewer, is ranked #10 of 10 - DEAD LAST in a closed ten-item pool** - while the
  engine's #1, Echoes of Helia, does not appear in her core at all. Confirmed at
  `top=200`, so it is not a truncation artifact. **The mechanism is complementarity:**
  `ds.hps` prices each item's healing in isolation, and by that measure Moonstone
  scores lowest; but Moonstone is bought precisely because its rebound converts her
  strictly single-target W into an AoE heal engine, i.e. it supplies what her kit
  LACKS. An objective that scores items standalone must rank a gap-filling item last.
  **MATCHED PAIR that isolates the shape from RM-91:** Tahm Kench and Taric are both
  `tank/ds.ehp` and receive the same list, but Tahm buys HP-as-damage and ZERO ally
  items (RM-91) while Taric's every ability terminates on a teammate and his armor is
  an INPUT to his healing output (RM-92). One scorer, two opposite champions, one
  answer. **Remedy class: RM-92 is L2 objective-coverage work like RM-91** - every fix
  is "this item must RISE" - and it is strictly larger, because RM-91 needs one
  missing term (HP -> damage) while RM-92 needs a family of them.
- **CANDIDACY FINDING (concrete, actionable, not a ranking gap): the carry pool is a
  fixed 111 items, byte-identical across seven marksmen** (Senna / Smolder / Sivir /
  Jinx / Caitlyn / Samira / Ashe), and it **categorically excludes Black Cleaver,
  Spear of Shojin, Bloodsong, Stridebreaker and Sterak's Gage** while admitting
  Eclipse. Measured at `top=200`, so this is genuine non-candidacy and not the
  truncation artifact corrected above. The exclusion is defensible for a crit-and-
  attack-speed marksman and wrong for the ability-led ones: it removes **Senna's
  26.7% opener** and **Smolder's entire 2nd-and-3rd core** from consideration before
  any scoring happens. Unlike RM-92 this is a filter-list question, not an objective
  question, so it may be the cheapest real win available from this sweep.
- batch21 (2026-07-18): Briar [GAP RM-39 family], Rumble [REFUTE], Ryze [GAP RM-70
  family], Samira [REFUTE], Sejuani [GAP RM-91, NEW].
- batch22 (2026-07-18): Senna [GAP RM-86 family], Seraphine [GAP RM-90 family],
  Sett [GAP RM-91 + RM-39], Shaco [GAP RM-86 family], Shen [GAP RM-91, canonical].
- batch23 (2026-07-18): Shyvana [GAP RM-39 family], Singed [GAP RM-91 + pool
  partition], Sion [GAP RM-91], Sivir [GAP RM-86 family], Skarner [GAP RM-91 + RM-87].
  **Briar was a HOLE, not a new champion** - roster line 289 carried `- [ ] Briar`
  with no verdict and no FENCED note, silently skipped by the B-batch. Swept first
  in batch21 so the strict-alphabetical claim is true again. Worth a standing check:
  the pending-count grep would never have caught this, because a hole and a
  not-yet-swept champion are the same string.
- **RM-91 NOTE (NEW SHAPE) - bonus HP is scored as EHP-only, so kits that CONVERT
  health into damage are systematically undervalued.** Five of this batch's fifteen
  champions carry it - Sejuani, Sett, Shen, Sion, Skarner - and two independent
  meta-research agents that never saw each other's output reached the identical
  conclusion unprompted ("an engine that scores Heartsteel as pure EHP will
  undervalue it here specifically" / "any scorer that classifies HP items as pure
  EHP will systematically undervalue Sett" / "an engine that scores Titanic Hydra as
  fighter DPS or scores HP as pure EHP will get Shen's first item wrong in both
  directions"). The mechanism is one-directional and that is what makes it
  diagnosable: `ds.ehp` prices the EHP term of Heartsteel / Titanic Hydra /
  Overlord's Bloodmail and none of the damage term, so pure-resist items
  (Randuin's, Warmog's) outrank HP-to-damage items on every champion, whether or
  not the kit spends HP offensively. Measured consequence: **Randuin's Omen is
  engine #1 for all five, and appears in the real core of NONE of them** (Skarner
  10.09% ninth item, Sejuani/Shen/Singed/Sion absent). Shen is the canonical
  instance - his ~27.3% near-universal opener Titanic Hydra sits #23 and his locked
  second item Dusk and Dawn is #51 of 143. Per the RM-86 L1 correction,
  a monotone-lowering sort-key gate CANNOT fix this: every remedy here is of the
  form "champion X's real item must RISE", so RM-91 is L2 objective-coverage work.
- **RM-90 RE-MEASURED - the cohort is far wider than RM-90 recorded, and GROUP B was
  mis-attributed.** Verified against the shipped artifact
  `data/daemon_slayer/16.14.1/build_orders_sr.json` (`balanced` variant, DISPLAY-keyed):
  RM-90 recorded "TWO byte-identical build orders across 12 champions" scoped to the
  SUPPORT class. Actual measurement: **GROUP A spans 10 champions, not 8** (adds Ivern
  and Zilean), and **GROUP B spans 28, not 4** - Alistar, Amumu, Bard, Blitzcrank,
  Braum, Cho'Gath, Dr. Mundo, Galio, K'Sante, Leona, Malphite, Maokai, Nautilus,
  Nunu & Willump, Ornn, Poppy, Rakan, Rammus, Rell, Sejuani, Shen, Singed, Sion,
  Skarner, Tahm Kench, Taric, Thresh, Zac. **GROUP B was never a support cohort at
  all: it is the generic TANK template, which happens to catch four supports.**
  Roster-wide the artifact is archetype-templated rather than per-champion - **53
  distinct orders for 173 champions, 140 of 173 (81%) sharing an order with at least
  one other champion, only 33 (19%) unique, largest cohort 28.** First-legendary
  histogram measured at ENGINE 1.219.0 / 16.14.1: **BotRK 64 / Liandry's 47 /
  Randuin's 28 / Echoes of Helia 11 = 150 of 173 (87%) on four items**. That
  supersedes the PRECOMPUTE HISTOGRAM figures carried in memory
  (BotRK 62 / Liandry's 53 / Randuin's 24 / Helia 16), which were measured at an
  earlier engine version; the shape of the claim is unchanged, the numbers moved.
  The `ap_heavy` variant swaps only the tank head (Randuin's -> Kaenic Rookern), so
  the variants are axis-responsive but not champion-responsive.
- **PROBE-DEPTH CORRECTION (2026-07-18, found while sweeping batch24/25 - applies
  to THIS batch and to every earlier batch that used the standing recipe).** The
  sweep's probe recipe calls `rank_for_primary_archetype(..., top=40)`, so "absent
  from the top-40" is a **TRUNCATION**, not a statement about the candidate pool.
  Measured at top=200 the real pools are **carry 111, bruiser/tank 143, mage 144**,
  and **all 118 purchasable terminal SR legendaries >=1500g are candidates
  somewhere** across the six archetypes. The affected wordings in this batch have
  been corrected in place: Sivir's Navori is **#28 of 111** (not missing), Sett's
  Bloodmail / Shojin / Sterak's are **#53 / #57 / #41 of 143**, Singed's Liandry's
  is **#49 of 143**, Shen's Dusk and Dawn is **#51**, Shyvana's Shojin **#49**.
  **The RETRACTED claim is the cross-axis POOL PARTITION shape**, which asserted
  that no route contains a hybrid champion's full core and that a Slice-C route
  override therefore could not help. All three of Singed's core items are
  candidates on his tank route. What survives is a RANKING gap already covered by
  RM-86 and RM-91, so no new spec number is spent on it. **Use `top=200` from
  batch24 onward, and never write "absent" without having probed past the
  truncation.**
- **GENUINELY absent items, and the two classes they fall into.** Only four of the
  items checked are truly not candidates, and the distinction matters because one
  class is a defect and the other is correct. **Defect class:** Black Cleaver and
  Bloodsong are not candidates on Senna's carry route, and Spear of Shojin is not a
  candidate on carry, though Shojin does enter tank (#35) and mage (#37-#40) - a
  bruiser-class item reachable from neither carry nor bruiser is worth a look on its
  own. **Correct-by-design class:** Seraph's Embrace and Fimbulwinter both carry
  `gold.purchasable` False, being Tear-line TRANSFORM targets, and are excluded on
  exactly the rationale the enchanter guard test applies to Diadem of Songs. Do NOT
  file either as an omission - and note this cuts against the first framing of the
  Ryze and Taliyah mana-stack verdicts, whose real evidence is the buried
  **purchasable** Rod of Ages, not the absent transform target.
- **DATA-QUALITY WARNING on `data/meta_build/sr_champion_builds.json`.** It can be
  wrong about which build is PRIMARY, not merely stale. It lists Shyvana's core as
  Kraken Slayer / Dusk and Dawn / Sundered Sky - her **5.06%** secondary path - while
  the real primary is Trinity Force / Spear of Shojin / Death's Dance at **35.43%**.
  This batch's first engine probe was run against that wrong build and had to be
  redone after the research agent contradicted it. Keep using the table as a cheap
  in-repo cross-check, but it does NOT substitute for the meta-research agent.
- batch20 (2026-07-18): Rell [GAP RM-90], Renata Glasc [GAP RM-90], Renekton [GAP RM-39],
  Rengar [GAP RM-39], Riven [GAP RM-39]. **Five GAPs, zero REFUTEs - the first
  all-GAP batch.** RM-90 is a COHORT verdict, not a per-champion one: the support
  class ships TWO byte-identical build orders across 12 champions, and the earlier
  Alistar / Blitzcrank / Braum / Bard / Rakan REFUTEs are now judged too lenient.
  The other three are RM-39 family and add the fourth and fifth corroborations that
  an RM-86 L1 conversion vector must be continuous rather than boolean. Also banked
  here: **batch19's Opportunity "defect" was WRONG and is retracted** - see the
  Rengar entry.
- batch19 (2026-07-18): Pyke [GAP RM-88, NEW], Quinn [GAP RM-89, NEW], Rakan [REFUTE],
  Rammus [GAP RM-87, canonical], Rek'Sai [GAP RM-39 family]. Qiyana was skipped as
  already FENCED. **Rammus displaces Ornn as RM-87's canonical example** (Thornmail
  92% pick at #20; three items under 1.6% combined pick at #2/#5/#9). **Quinn is the
  first champion to carry RC-1 AND RC-2 at once** and is the decisive test case for
  the RM-86 L1 plan - see her verdict. A separate engine defect surfaced via Quinn
  (the legendary Opportunity enters ZERO ranked pools for anyone) is filed as its
  own task, NOT fixed here.
- batch18 (2026-07-18): Olaf [GAP RM-39 family], Orianna [GAP RM-40 family],
  Ornn [GAP RM-87, NEW shape], Pantheon [GAP RM-39 family], Poppy [REFUTE].
  Only ONE new spec number in five champions - three of the four GAPs land in
  families already characterised, which is the same convergence batch17 found.
  The batch headline is the **Ornn / Poppy matched pair**: same route
  (tank/ds.ehp), near-identical engine list, OPPOSITE verdicts - see the
  batch18 method note below.

## Data-currency caveat (RM-81) - READ BEFORE TRUSTING ANY VERDICT

RM-81 was found during batch15 on two champions and has since been MEASURED across
the whole roster by `tools/ds_wiki_staleness_check.py --full`, which diffs stored
base-damage and cooldown endpoints against the live wiki Data templates. Result
(patch 16.14.1, report at `data/daemon_slayer/16.14.1/ability_staleness.json`):

- **75 of 171 champions (44%) carry at least one drifted ability value**, 123 findings.
- **39 of the 86 champions already given a sweep verdict (45%) are among them** - Ahri,
  Akshan, Anivia, Annie, Azir, Blitzcrank, Brand, Braum, Camille, Cassiopeia, Corki,
  Draven, Fizz, Galio, Gangplank, Garen, Gnar, Graves, Gwen, Hecarim, Heimerdinger,
  Hwei, Irelia, Jhin, Jinx, Kassadin, Kayle, Kayn, Kennen, Lissandra, Lucian, Lulu,
  Lux, Malphite, Malzahar, Maokai, Mel, Mordekaiser, Varus.
- **33 of the 84 still-pending champions** are affected too, including the next batch
  (Morgana, Naafiri, Nami, Nasus).

RECALL IS MEASURED, not assumed. Riot's own patch notes (reachable directly and
URL-predictable, e.g. `.../news/game-updates/league-of-legends-patch-26-14-notes/`,
with the champion list in `h3.change-title`) give an authoritative "who changed in
patch N" set. Cross-checked against 26.14: the detector catches **7 of the 9**
checkable champions, and both misses are correct BY-DESIGN exclusions rather than
bugs - Seraphine's change was an AP RATIO (50% -> 40%, which is the CDragon ratio
report's job) and Jayce's was a defensive RESIST GRANT, neither being a damage base
or a cooldown. So recall on in-scope fields is 7/7. Locke is correctly absent, being
the RM-79 missing-champion case. That cross-check also FOUND a real recall gap since
fixed: pages that hoist numbers into MediaWiki `#vardefine` variables (Garen's R is
`{{ap|{{#var:b1}} to {{#var:b3}}}}`) parsed to nothing, hiding Riot's 26.14 cut of
150/250/350 -> 125/200/275; resolving those variables recovered 4 champions and 14
findings.

This does NOT auto-invalidate those verdicts: the sweep's bright line compares the
engine's ITEM ORDER against real-meta build data, and most findings are modest
base/cooldown deltas that shift magnitudes rather than the kit AXIS. But a verdict
that turned on a damage MAGNITUDE (front-loaded-vs-sustained splits, %maxHP weight,
burst-window arithmetic) should be re-checked against the report before being
treated as settled. `champion_ability_data_is_current()` exposes the same signal at
runtime, and `--recent` re-checks changed champions daily via RC-UpstreamDriftCheck.

Cause is upstream and unchanged: Meraki's `latest` endpoint is frozen at content
patch 25.15 (deliberately pinned + logged). The gap is **23 patches, not the ~11
this line claimed until 2026-07-18** - measured directly off Riot's own patch-note
chain in `docs/specs/DECISION_riot_patch_note_backfill.md`.

## Resolved verdicts

- **Smolder** - GAP RM-92 + a CANDIDACY gap (**an ability-DPS champion routed to the auto-attack-DPS scorer, whose 2nd and 3rd core items are not candidates on that route at all**) - real meta = bot/ADC mono-role, 4.28% pick, 50.92-52.69% WR (~23,400-30,031 games), **Deathfire Touch ~87.4-90%**, Gluttonous Greaves 43.51% over attack-speed boots. Signature = **Essence Reaver 97.14% presence and a true mono-opener** -> Black Cleaver -> Spear of Shojin at 37.3% pick / 56.79% WR, then Rapid Firecannon. Production carry/ds.dps leads **BotRK #1 and Runaan's Hurricane #2, neither in his core**, and although it places Essence Reaver **#3 of 111** - a genuine near-hit on his opener - **Black Cleaver and Spear of Shojin are NOT CANDIDATES on the carry route at all** (Rapid Firecannon #32, IE #7, Lord Dominik's #9). **Measured as a filter rule, not a per-champion accident:** the carry pool is a fixed **111 items, byte-identical across Senna / Smolder / Sivir / Jinx / Caitlyn / Samira / Ashe**, and it categorically excludes Black Cleaver, Spear of Shojin, Bloodsong, Stridebreaker and Sterak's Gage while admitting Eclipse. That exclusion is defensible for a crit-and-attack-speed marksman and wrong for the ability-led ones - it removes Senna's 26.7% opener and Smolder's entire 2nd-and-3rd core from consideration. His damage is Q-driven with a stack-threshold execute, so the binding constraint is ability haste plus mana-per-auto, not auto-attack throughput; Gluttonous Greaves beating attack-speed boots is the independent tell. Rapid Firecannon is bought for Energized attack RANGE (safe passive-stacking), not crit. Note against the guide prose: Infinity Edge appears in only ~3% of core-3 builds, so the "late-game crit monster" framing is not in the data. RM-81: STALE.
- **Sona** - GAP RM-92 + RM-90 family (**her single most-built item is not a candidate, and the mana-to-heal-power engine that defines her is unmodelled**) - real meta = support mono-role, 3.32% pick, 51.99-53.05% WR, **Summon Aery 94.3% row-summed and verified three ways** (a genuine mono-keystone in the Shen class, not a tree total). Signature = Bloodsong -> Boots of Swiftness 49.03% -> the Tear line into Winter's Approach / Archangel's -> Echoes of Helia / Staff of Flowing Water -> **Diadem of Songs** -> Moonstone or Dawncore. Presence: **Bloodsong 53.09% (her most-built item)**, Echoes 52.54%, Moonstone 35.94%, Diadem 35.58%, Dream Maker 32.37%, Staff 26.33%. Production enchanter/ds.hps returns Echoes **#1 of a closed 10-item pool** - matching her second item, but by the proven champion-invariance rather than by reading her kit, since **Soraka receives this identical list** (see her entry). **Bloodsong, Diadem of Songs and Dream Maker are all outside the pool**; Dream Maker and Diadem are the guard-tested by-design exclusions, but **Bloodsong is the RM-92 case and it is the interesting one** - on Sona it is not a personal-damage item at all. Its on-hit applies Expose Weakness, roughly +8% to all damage the target takes **from any source**, and because she has the highest cast cadence of any enchanter she holds near-permanent Spellblade uptime, i.e. she is running a team-wide damage-amplification aura. A self-throughput objective prices that at zero. Her actual scaling stat is MANA: Winter's Approach / Fimbulwinter feeds Diadem, whose passive converts bonus mana into heal-and-shield power - an entire conversion chain with no term in the engine. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Soraka** - GAP RM-92 (**NEW SHAPE and the CANONICAL instance - her 90.28%-presence near-mandatory first legendary is ranked DEAD LAST in the engine's entire pool**) - real meta = support 97.8% of games, 4.40% pick, 50.39-51.99% WR (23,199-29,464 games), a genuine two-keystone champion (**Summon Aery 78.8%** row-summed, **Guardian 18.5%**), Ionian Boots 67.91%. Signature = **Moonstone Renewer, 90.28% presence, leading all five top ordered cores** -> Redemption 44.08% -> Dawncore 24.78%, with Ardent Censer 13.9%. Production enchanter/ds.hps returns **Echoes of Helia #1 - an item absent from her core entirely - and ranks Moonstone Renewer #10 of 10, last**. Redemption #6, Dawncore #9, Ardent #2. This is a total inversion inside a CLOSED pool, and it is not a truncation artifact: the pool really is ten items at `top=200`. **The mechanism is the finding.** `ds.hps` prices each item by its own healing throughput in isolation, and by that measure Moonstone scores lowest. But Moonstone is bought precisely because its ramping rebound converts Soraka's strictly single-target W into an AoE heal engine - **it supplies the thing her kit LACKS**. The engine cannot represent complementarity, so the item that fills the gap ranks last while items that merely add raw HPS rank first. Her ability-haste boots at 67.91% are the corroborating tell: her throughput is literally a function of casts per minute. **Encode the contrast with Sona:** Soraka does NOT build Diadem of Songs and Sona does, correctly, because Soraka scales on AP and heal power rather than on mana pool - two enchanters with genuinely different scaling that the engine hands one identical list. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Swain** - GAP RM-92 (**his 86.41% signature is a CC-uptime purchase, which no self-output objective can price**) - real meta is **the batch's TRAP-D case: his most-played role is his only losing one.** Lane split support 44% / bot 26% / top 16% / mid 14%, and support wins **47.45%** while bot wins ~52.0% - a win-rate-anchored reading would have picked the wrong primary, so this entry is anchored to pick share and the inversion recorded. Champion-wide 2.70% pick. Keystone required a row-sum and flipped the answer: the largest single ROW is Conqueror+Resolve 35.94%, but **Electrocute summed across four rows is 46.8%** and is the real plurality. Signature (support) = **Rylai's Crystal Scepter 86.41% presence, leading all five top cores** -> Liandry's -> Zhonya's 33.1%. Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's #1 of 144** (in his build, but as his second item, not his opener) and **Blackfire Torch #2, which is not in his build at all**, while **Rylai's sits #25**. Rylai's on Swain is not a damage item: his R is a persistent AoE damage aura, so the slow re-applies continuously to everything inside it, converting the drain into a permanent AoE slow field - a CC-uptime buy. Liandry's monetises the same persistent tick, and Zhonya's buys the stasis that makes his mandatory melee commitment survivable. **Second, separable finding worth a probe of its own:** the archetype-correct sustain items are underplayed by the meta AND buried further by the engine - Spirit Visage is **#82 of 144** and Unending Despair **#66**, yet the Rylai's->Zhonya's->Spirit Visage line (58.1%) and ->Liandry's line (58.22%) both beat the most-popular Liandry's-first line (49.58%). Those are combo-level and lower-sample, so it is a hypothesis rather than a proven ranking - but the engine is on the wrong side of it. Research also notes Electrocute is archetype-incoherent for a sustained-tick drain champion and offers it as a plausible mechanical explanation for the 47.45%. RM-81: STALE.
- **Sylas** - GAP RM-92 (**his 84.72% near-mandatory opener is a MOBILITY purchase at #28 of 143**) - real meta = mid primary, 9.22% pick and a **19.56% ban rate**, 50.66-52.06% WR (49,399-63,370 games). Keystone is a genuine two-way split, **Conqueror 54-57%** row-summed against **Electrocute 41.8%**, and this is the case that resolves the decode hazard cleanly: rune id 8010 is Conqueror in Precision, NOT the item of the same numeric id. Signature = **Hextech Rocketbelt 84.72% presence** -> Riftmaker 45.95% -> Zhonya's 43.91% at 24.18% pick / 53.09% WR. Production mage/ds.ability leads Liandry's #1 (not in his core) and Blackfire #2 (not in his core), placing **Rocketbelt #28 of 143** while getting Riftmaker #10, Zhonya's #12 and Shadowflame #5 broadly right. Rocketbelt is bought as a second mobility charge for a melee AP champion with exactly one native dash - engage and disengage, with the AP and haste incidental. A self-damage objective prices a dash at zero. **The archetype call matters and is easy to get wrong:** he looks like a burst assassin and is not one - W heals more the lower he is, his ultimate steals an enemy ultimate rather than multiplying his own burst, and **Conqueror at 54-57% ramps over a long fight and rewards nothing about a burst rotation**. Both his majority keystone and his majority item core say sustained AP skirmisher. Lich Bane at 14% is a niche side-lane path on a champion with no auto-reset, not a core item. Discard the Dark Seal openers (6.6% / 63.54% and 2.8% / 61.53%) and Mejai's at 82.23% - Dark Seal is a 350g component bought before the first legendary, and those are textbook snowball artifacts. RM-81: STALE.
- **Syndra** - GAP RM-40 / RM-63 family (**a single-target burst mage handed the invariant DoT mage head; no new spec number**) - real meta = mid primary 8.06% pick / 50.68% WR (43,635 games) with a real bot secondary at 28% of her SR volume and the SAME AP build, **First Strike 43.94%** (Electrocute 15.30%, Arcane Comet 14.47%), Sorcerer's Shoes 62.56%. Signature = **Luden's Echo 58.0%** -> Shadowflame 51.41% -> Rabadon's Deathcap 40.31%, with Blackfire Torch 39.30% the alternate opener - slot-1 is a clean two-horse race summing to 97.3%. Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's Torment #1 of 144, an item that does not appear in her core at any rate**, and buries **Luden's Echo at #13** while ranking Rabadon's #4 and Shadowflame #5 correctly. Her pattern is a Q-E stun-flick into a flat-AP-ratio ultimate - one rotation, single target, no on-hit and no auto-attack scaling - so the build buys ability haste plus FLAT MAGIC PENETRATION to make one rotation lethal (Sorcerer's 62.6% and Shadowflame 51.4% are both flat-pen buys). A sustained-DoT lead is the wrong axis for her, which is exactly the Ahri RM-40 / Blackfire RM-63 fingerprint. **She is also the second confirmed Luden's Echo champion after Mel**, further breaking the old "Luden's appears ZERO times" claim. Excellent worked example of the baseline artifact to reuse in training: **Luden's has the LOWEST win rate on her sheet (50.62%) despite the HIGHEST presence (58%)** - a near-universal first item is present in every loss too, so its WR collapses to the champion mean, while low-presence late items float. RM-81: STALE.
- **Tahm Kench** - GAP RM-91 (**HP bought as a damage stat; he is the deliberate contrast case to Taric below**) - real meta = genuinely two-role, **support 58% (13,149 games, 50.95% WR) / top 42% (9,782, 49.52%)**, both HP-stacking and neither AD- nor AP-primary. **Grasp of the Undying ~97.3% top** - suspected as a trap-A mis-parse, triple-checked (97.3% summed rows, 98.2% headline, 98.3% from a second aggregator) and confirmed genuine. Signature both roles = **Heartsteel, 81.92% presence top / 75.76% support, opening all three leading combos in each**; top then locks **Dusk and Dawn 58.57%** at slot 2, support goes Thornmail -> Spirit Visage. Production tank/ds.ehp leads **Randuin's Omen #1, not in either role's core**, and places Heartsteel **#5**, Spirit Visage #8, Unending Despair #9, Thornmail #21, Titanic Hydra #23. **The valuation is unambiguous and research states it directly: this build buys HP-as-damage, not raw EHP.** Heartsteel is the only item on the sheet whose entire proposition is converting stacked health into burst, and it is picked over Sunfire Aegis (9.5%) and Hollow Radiance (8.4%), which are the pure-EHP choices - his passive stacks bonus magic damage on autos and W, and Q and W both carry health scaling. Riftmaker at 13.78% top is the confirming oddity: an AP omnivamp item on a tank, bought because its health plus ramping amp compounds with the HP-scaling passive. **He buys essentially ZERO ally-facing utility** - no Knight's Vow, no Locket, no Redemption; his one "team" item is the mandated support-quest upgrade, not a choice. Two artifacts quarantined: Bramble Vest 19.68% / 46.64% is an unupgraded Thornmail component (trap C, short losing games), and Riftmaker's 60.90% is third-slot survivorship. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Taliyah** - GAP RM-70 family + role-divergence (**two roles that buy different things and share one template**) - real meta = mid primary (7,559-7,883 games, 1.38% pick, 49.46% WR) / jungle secondary (3,663, 0.70%, 51.42%), split ~68/32, both AP. **Thinnest samples in this batch, so item tails past ~5% presence are unreliable and this verdict is recorded at MEDIUM confidence.** Mid signature = **Seraph's Embrace 44.46% -> Rylai's 43.46% -> Liandry's 28.63%**, Ionian Boots 50.39%; jungle signature is a different champion - **Blackfire Torch 79.23% -> Shadowflame 65.51% -> Zhonya's 53.87%**, Sorcerer's Shoes 86.28%, and **Seraph's collapses to 0.10% presence** because camps solve the mana problem. Production mage/ds.ability gives both roles the same head - Liandry's #1, Blackfire #2 - with **Rod of Ages #34** and **Rylai's #24**. **Correctly NOT filed as an omission:** Seraph's Embrace is genuinely not a candidate, but `gold.purchasable` is False (it is the Archangel's transform target), the same by-design exclusion the enchanter guard test applies to Diadem of Songs. Her real evidence is the buried **purchasable** Rod of Ages, exactly as for Ryze - so she is the third mana-stack-signature champion after Kassadin and Ryze, and RM-70 needs no new number. Mid buys mana throughput plus hit-rate (Rylai's makes W and Q land; her AP-per-gold is poor by design); jungle buys burn plus penetration. **Independent finding worth surfacing rather than encoding: her most-picked mid keystone is her worst-performing one** - Deathfire Touch 37.84% at 46.85% WR sits ~2.6 points BELOW her 49.46% baseline while Stormraider's Surge 23.79% sits ~2.7 above, which looks like Season-2 new-rune over-adoption. Artifacts quarantined: Mejai's 11.71% / 86.78% and Oblivion Orb 5.34% / 40.58% (trap C). RM-81: STALE.
- **Taric** - GAP RM-92 (**the ally-facing pole of the shape, and the exact inverse of Tahm Kench - the two together are the cleanest matched pair in the batch**) - real meta = support 95.7% of volume, 1.18% pick and **53.29% WR, the strongest baseline of the ten**, **Glacial Augment ~67.1%** row-summed and triple-corroborated, boots the most fragmented of any champion here (no option clears 41%). Signature = **Locket of the Iron Solari 69.82%, the first completed legendary in every leading sequence** -> Fimbulwinter 43.45% -> Knight's Vow 29.23%, with Frozen Heart 18.65%, Bandlepipes 11.75%, Zeke's Convergence 9.10%. Production tank/ds.ehp leads Randuin's #1 and places **Locket #25, Zeke's #18, Bandlepipes #31, Knight's Vow #32 and Echoes of Helia #65 of 143** - his entire build is present but buried under thirty-odd items he never buys. **Every ability he has terminates on a teammate:** Q heals in an area off his own ARMOR, W permanently links an ally and grants them a share of his armor while letting him cast Q and E through them, R is team-wide invulnerability. **The modelling point that makes him RM-92 rather than RM-91: armor and mana are load-bearing HEAL-THROUGHPUT stats for him, not merely EHP** - an engine pricing Frozen Heart on Taric as pure EHP undervalues it, because armor is an INPUT to his healing output. Set against Tahm Kench, who buys zero ally items and converts HP to damage, the pair shows a single scorer (`ds.ehp`) handing two opposite champions one list. **Correctly NOT filed as an omission:** Fimbulwinter is not a candidate but `gold.purchasable` is False - it is the Winter's Approach transform target. Trap-C artifact quarantined: Winter's Approach 2.54% / 44.85% is the unupgraded precursor, and Knight's Vow 61.02% / Bandlepipes 61.26% are third/fourth-slot survivorship not comparable to Locket's 53.58%. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Teemo** - GAP RM-86 family, a ROUTING defect (**an on-hit champion on the pure-AP mage template, with his 80.91% opener at #39 of 144**) - real meta = **top primary 74.3% (21,423 games, 3.81% pick, 51.24% WR)**, jungle secondary ~10.6% and **losing at 46.80%**. **Press the Attack ~73.9%** row-summed - the auto-attack-centric confirmation. **The AP-vs-on-hit framing this batch was dispatched with is now WRONG and the correction is the headline:** the split has collapsed in top lane because the dominant opener is ITSELF a hybrid on-hit item. **Statikk Shiv at 16.14 is 40 AD / 45 AP / 30% attack speed** with an Electrospark on-hit proc, and it carries **80.91% presence**; dedicated on-hit (Nashor's 15.77%) is a supplementary pickup, and pure on-hit (Guinsoo's 1.12%, Wit's End 1.13%, Terminus absent) is functionally dead under 2.5% combined. Signature = **Statikk Shiv -> Liandry's Torment 73.70% -> Malignance 54.48% at 38.64% pick / 55.89% WR**, the most concentrated single path in the batch. Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's #1 - which is his real SECOND item - while ranking his actual opener Statikk Shiv #39 of 144** (Malignance #16, Nashor's #19). Two compounding causes, both stated by research: he is routed to a pure-AP archetype template despite being an on-hit-plus-DoT champion, **and any scorer pricing Statikk Shiv by its 40 AD component mis-ranks it, because Teemo has no AD ratios anywhere in his kit** - that AD is dead stat weight bought for the attack speed and the proc. Statikk Shiv and Liandry's are a single mechanism, not two damage sources: constant on-hit application converted into percent-max-health burn, with Malignance buying ultimate haste for shroom uptime. Note the engine DOES ship a `ds.onhit` scorer (Slice B) that he never reaches. Survivorship flags: Rabadon's 60.10% and Shadowflame 57.29% are fourth/fifth-slot at low presence and are NOT better than Statikk Shiv's 51.72%. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Briar** - GAP RM-39 family (ability/lethality-bruiser mis-led by the ds.hybrid on-hit head; **she was a silent HOLE in the roster, not a new champion** - line 289 carried `- [ ] Briar` with no verdict and no FENCED note, skipped by the B-batch, so the alphabetical claim was false until now) - real meta = jungle 89.5% (28,768 role games, 51.60% WR, 4.85% pick), Press the Attack 51.9%. Signature opener **The Collector 69.5% of slot-1 (67.08% presence)** -> Titanic Hydra 42.08% -> Lord Dominik's 35.17%, Death's Dance 32.56%, Black Cleaver 25.68%. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid leads **BotRK #1 - which is her 20.08% SIXTH item** - then Trinity Force, Heartsteel, Essence Reaver; her 69.5% opener sits **#29**, Titanic Hydra **#27**, Black Cleaver **#31**, and Death's Dance is **#41 of 143 - one position past the probe truncation, which is exactly why the depth correction below was needed**. Ability data present and CURRENT (RM-81 clean). **Second finding, independent of the scorer:** the source data splits her subrole **FIGHTER 53.1% / MARKSMAN 41.2%** - one champion id genuinely running two different builds - so a single archetype template cannot score her even with a perfect objective.
- **Rumble** - REFUTE (**flagged non-lenient: the #1 is correct AND mechanistically right, but two of the engine's top four are items he never buys - recorded so this is not re-read later as a clean pass**) - real meta = top 83.7% (9,223 role games, 49.02% WR), Deathfire Touch 65.6%, Sorcerer's Shoes 45.72%. Signature = **Liandry's Torment 92.14% presence / 94.5% of slot-1** -> Bloodletter's Curse 39.01% -> Zhonya's Hourglass 37.96%, Shadowflame 27.55%, Riftmaker 13.70%. Production mage/ds.ability returns **Liandry's #1 - correct, and correct for the right reason** (his kit is continuous re-applied burn, so a percent-max-HP DoT lead is the true valuation, not a coincidence of the template). All seven real core items land in the top-28 and five in the top-12 (Bloodletter's #9, Zhonya's #12, Shadowflame #5, Riftmaker #10) - the best real-item coverage in this batch. **The defect that keeps this from being clean:** Blackfire Torch **#2** does not appear in his real core at all, and Rabadon's Deathcap **#4** is a **7.66%** item - research names that low Rabadon's number as the single most informative datapoint on him, because his damage is percent-health and duration-based rather than AP-ratio-based. So the head is right and the 2nd/4th slots are the invariant mage template asserting itself over his kit. Ability data CURRENT.
- **Ryze** - GAP RM-70 family (**the SECOND mana-stack-signature champion; Kassadin took the number, Ryze corroborates it and is the more extreme case**) - real meta = mid 80.2% (17,739 role games, 47.23% WR - genuinely weak this patch), Deathfire Touch 60.7%, Mercury's Treads 40.48% (bought as damage uptime; he has no dash). Signature is a single path at **68.49% of cored games**: Tear of the Goddess rush (94.3% of slot-1) -> **Rod of Ages 94.38%** -> **Seraph's Embrace 86.98%** -> Actualizer 53.00%; Rabadon's, the archetypal AP item, sits at only **15.92%**. Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's Torment #1 - an item that does not appear in his real core at any pick rate** - with Rabadon's **#3** and Void Staff #4, while **Rod of Ages is #24**, and Seraph's Embrace is genuinely not a candidate - but **correctly so**, since `gold.purchasable` is False (it is the Archangel's transform target, the same by-design exclusion the guard test applies to Diadem of Songs), so it must NOT be read as an engine omission. His Q scales off MAX MANA in addition to AP, so max mana is a damage stat on this kit and the engine has no term for it: it prices the two items he buys in 87-94% of games below twenty items he never buys. Ability data CURRENT, and the verdict turns on item SET rather than a damage magnitude, so RM-81 does not bear on it.

Signature = **The Collector 97.16% presence / 90.0% of slot-1** -> Infinity Edge 85.80% -> Lord Dominik's 47.11%, Immortal Shieldbow 41.82%. Production carry/ds.dps returns **Lord Dominik's #1, The Collector #2, Infinity Edge #3** - the correct three-item set - with Bloodthirster #5, Hubris #7, Mortal Reminder #12, Shieldbow #14. **Footnote defect (mild RM-87 intra-pool weighting, NOT enough to overturn):** the head order is inverted - her 47.11% Lord Dominik's leads her 97.16%-presence / 90%-opener Collector. **RM-81 status: Samira is STALE** (`champion_ability_data_is_current` returns False - Q base Physical Damage stored 5-25 vs live 0-20, R Inferno Trigger per-shot stored 5-25 vs live 20-60, a large drift). This verdict is recorded as SAFE against that because it rests on which items are ranked, not on any burst-window arithmetic; **do not reuse this entry to support a damage-magnitude claim about Samira.** Independent corroboration of the archetype read: Berserker's Greaves is at **1.07%** - a marksman all but abandoning attack-speed boots is hard evidence she is not a sustained-AS carry.
- **Sejuani** - GAP RM-91 (**NEW SHAPE - see the RM-91 note below; bonus HP scored as EHP-only on a kit that converts HP into damage**) - real meta = jungle 81.4%, subrole TANK 98.4% (10,067 role games, 49.90% WR), Aftershock 82.9%. Signature = **Heartsteel 69.31% presence / 85.7% of slot-1** -> Unending Despair 39.38% / Thornmail 39.45% -> Kaenic Rookern 28.76%, Sunfire Aegis 25.53%, Jak'Sho 16.62%. Production tank/ds.ehp leads **Randuin's Omen #1 - an item that does not appear anywhere in her real core set** - and buries her 85.7% opener at **#5** (Thornmail #20, Sunfire #11, Protoplasm #23). Her detonation damage keys off target max health and her abilities scale off BONUS HEALTH, so a health stack raises damage and durability at once; research states the conclusion independently and in the same words the engine needs - "an engine that scores Heartsteel as pure EHP will undervalue it here specifically". **Second, separable finding:** Zeke's Convergence 10.24% and Knight's Vow 2.55% on a JUNGLER are real ally-buff purchases that neither a self-DPS nor a self-EHP objective can price. RM-81: Sejuani is STALE (Q cooldown 19-13 stored vs 18-12 live, R 130-90 vs 120-90) - immaterial here, the verdict is an item-order claim.

Support signature = **Black Cleaver (26.7% of slot-1)** -> Statikk Shiv -> Rapid Firecannon, support item Bloodsong 84.07%; bot flips the first two (Statikk Shiv 52% opener) and adds a Tear/Muramana line at 14.55%. Boots are the loudest tell: **Boots of Swiftness 89.05%** over Berserker's. Black Cleaver is not missing from the ENGINE - it is **#5 on her mage reroute and #11 on assassin** - so this is a pool-partition defect, not an item-registry gap: no single archetype pool contains her real core. Her passive converts soul pickups into permanent AD, crit, range and lifesteal, which is exactly why the meta declines to buy crit and the sustained-DPS scorer insists on it. RM-81: STALE (Q 30-150 stored vs 30-130 live, W 70-250 vs 70-230) - immaterial to an item-set verdict.
- **Seraphine** - GAP RM-90 family (**she is a named member of RM-90 GROUP A, which this batch re-measures at 10 champions rather than 8**) - real meta = **support 72.6% / bot 25.4%**; correcting the batch brief, her secondary role is BOT, not mid (mid is 1.9% of games, 0.2% pick, 48.8% WR - not modellable). Support 63,865-76,089 games, 50.6-52.1% WR, ranked #3 of 81. Support signature = Tear of the Goddess -> **Echoes of Helia** -> **Diadem of Songs** -> Moonstone Renewer, Ionian Boots 91.72%; bot signature is a different champion entirely - Seraph's Embrace -> Imperial Mandate -> Rylai's - and **the keystone flips with the role, Summon Aery 67.2% support vs Arcane Comet 87.9% bot**. See `agents/daemon_slayer/tests/test_enchanter_pool_hsp_rm90.py`. The finding is therefore sharper than an omission: **a modern support build is dominated by items the ds.hps pool cannot represent by correct design**, so the #1 match carries no information about her. Signature = **Stridebreaker, the opener in all three leading combos (~45% aggregated)** -> Overlord's Bloodmail 61.72% / Black Cleaver -> Sterak's Gage 61.55%, Spear of Shojin 61.34-61.96%. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid leads Trinity Force #1 / Dusk and Dawn / Heartsteel / BotRK, and of his real core **Overlord's Bloodmail #53, Spear of Shojin #57 and Sterak's Gage #41 of a 143-item bruiser pool** while Stridebreaker sits **#36** and Black Cleaver **#38**. All three rank far higher on his TANK route (Bloodmail #27, Sterak's #20, Shojin #35), so the axis split is real - but it is a RANKING gap, not a candidacy gap. **Why it is RM-91 and not only a routing defect:** his W stores incoming damage as grit and releases it as true damage and his R scales with the TARGET's max HP, so durability is literally an offensive stat on this kit - research reaches the identical conclusion unprompted, that "any scorer that classifies HP items as pure EHP will systematically undervalue Sett". Note a route override alone would not fix the ORDER (his items sit #20-#36 even inside the tank pool), which per the RM-86 L1 correction makes the remedy L2 objective-coverage work, NOT an L1 lowering gate. RM-81: CURRENT.

AD jungle signature = **Voltaic Cyclosword (~12.9% of slot-1) or Youmuu's Ghostblade (~5.4%)** -> The Collector 55.73% -> Infinity Edge 58.99%, Lord Dominik's 59.07%. Production assassin/ds.burst leads **BotRK #1, Trinity Force #2, Essence Reaver #3, Lich Bane #4** - an on-hit/spellblade head on a champion whose damage is one guaranteed-crit backstab - while **Voltaic Cyclosword is ABSENT from the pool**, Youmuu's is **#31** and The Collector **#21**. **The AP variant is a second, cleaner failure:** at a real 4.41% jungle / dominant-in-support share it builds Blackfire Torch -> Imperial Mandate -> Liandry's, and **all three are ABSENT from the burst pool**; research notes AP Shaco is not an assassin build at all but a box-and-slow zone-control build, so routing it to ds.burst is wrong at the archetype level before any item is scored. RM-81: STALE (Q cooldown 12-10 stored vs 13-11 live) - immaterial to an item-set verdict.
- **Shen** - GAP RM-91 (**the canonical statement of the shape: his near-universal opener is #23 and his locked second item is #51 of a 143-item pool**) - real meta = top 65.4% / support 27.8% (top 20,387-24,782 games, 51.4-52.7% WR), **Grasp of the Undying 97.3%** (raw count 99.2% of sample crossed the trap-A tripwire and was retained only after the alternatives were confirmed individually enumerated and a second source agreed - treat the percentage as genuine and the raw count as soft). Signature = **Titanic Hydra, which opens all four leading combos (~27.3% aggregated, 53.36% presence)** -> **Dusk and Dawn (locked second)** -> Bandlepipes / Thornmail / Kaenic Rookern / Unending Despair as an interchangeable third. Production tank/ds.ehp leads Randuin's Omen #1 and places **Titanic Hydra #23, Dusk and Dawn #51 of 143, Bandlepipes #32**. His Q empowers autos with MAX-HP-scaling damage and his R is a global ally shield, so he buys HP once and spends it twice; research states the two-sided hazard exactly - "an engine that scores Titanic Hydra as fighter DPS or scores HP as pure EHP will get Shen's first item wrong in both directions". **Separable second finding:** his 27.8% support build abandons HP-to-damage entirely for team-wide mitigation (Locket -> Protoplasm -> Thornmail) and **flips the keystone to Aftershock 66.2%** - a different archetype on the same champion id, which no per-champion route can express. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Shyvana** - GAP RM-39 family (ability-haste bruiser led by the on-hit head) **and a DATA-QUALITY finding that matters beyond her** - real meta = jungle-primary (~3.2% pick, 51.9-53.5% WR, 17.6k-22.5k games), Press the Attack 68.1%. **The AP/AD question resolves decisively: AP Shyvana is effectively extinct at Emerald+** (no Nashor's Tooth anywhere in an any-slot table running down to a 2.76% cutoff; call it AD/bruiser 90%+, AP under 5%). Signature = **Trinity Force 65.56% -> Spear of Shojin 61.66% -> Death's Dance 42.21%, at 35.43% pick / 59.06% WR** - the most concentrated core in this batch. The build buys ability haste plus proc damage plus enough EHP to stay in melee - a bruiser curve, and Trinity and Shojin are both AH-and-proc items rather than raw AD. **DATA-QUALITY FINDING - act on this before trusting `sr_champion_builds.json` again:** RC's own scraped table lists her core as Kraken Slayer / Dusk and Dawn / Sundered Sky, which is the **5.06%-pick secondary path, not the 35.43% primary**. The first probe of this batch was run against that wrong build and had to be redone. The scraped table is a cheap cross-check, **not** a substitute for the meta-research agent, and it can be wrong about which build is primary rather than merely stale. RM-81: STALE, and hers is the most severe drift in the batch (E Flame Breath stored base 160-135 vs live 85-245 - the stored range is inverted in direction, not merely shifted).
