- **Singed** - GAP RM-91 + cross-axis pool partition (**the sharpest single case in the batch: his real first item ranks #49 on the route he is given, and the build he ships contains zero AP items**) - real meta = top 83.4% (15,303-18,500 games, 52.1-53.5% WR, a top-10-of-106 performer), **Deathfire Touch ~74-79%**, **Boots of Swiftness 63.33%** on a champion whose entire win condition is being uncatchable. Signature = **Liandry's Torment 86.32% -> Rylai's Crystal Scepter 81.41% -> Protoplasm Harness 37.10%**, with Dead Man's 21.86% and Riftmaker 14.43%. Production tank/ds.ehp leads Randuin's Omen #1 and ranks his 86.32% first item **Liandry's Torment #49 of 143**, with Rylai's at **#38** and Protoplasm at #22. He ships the 28-champion tank template (Randuin's -> Mercury's Treads -> Warmog's -> Sterak's -> Jak'Sho -> Spirit Visage), which contains **zero AP items** for a champion whose first two purchases are AP in 81-86% of games. **Measured across every route.** The tank route buries Liandry's at #49; the mage and bruiser routes lead Liandry's #1 but rank Protoplasm and Jak'Sho far lower. **CORRECTION 2026-07-18: an earlier wording of this entry claimed no pool CONTAINS all three of his core items and that a Slice-C route override therefore could not fix him. That was a top-40 truncation artifact and is RETRACTED** - all three are candidates on the tank route (#22 / #38 / #49). The defect is that his real core is ranked below thirty-odd items he never buys, which is RM-91 and RM-86, not a separate partition shape. Valuation: he buys neither raw EHP nor burst but percent-max-HP burn plus chase-lock (Rylai's converts every poison tick into a slow, which IS the engine of the kit), and only then EHP. Trap-B artifact quarantined: Mejai's 4.98% pick at 84.75% WR is a won-games-only selection artifact. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Sion** - GAP RM-91 (**and the batch's clearest case of the engine failing a champion who most needs it**) - real meta = top 74.3% / mid 15.7% (21,300-25,100 games, 49.2-50.7% WR - the weakest performer in the batch), **Grasp of the Undying 86.8%**, verified genuine two independent ways rather than auto-rejected on the tripwire. **His build is severely fragmented - the top ordered core sequence is only 4.53% of games** (against 35.43% for Shyvana): there is no consensus Sion build, only a consensus item POOL - Unending Despair 52.54%, Heartsteel 35.45%, Hollow Radiance 33.16%, Thornmail 27.36%, Titanic Hydra 24.98%, Sunfire 22.53%, Jak'Sho 16.92%, Overlord's Bloodmail 8.4%. Production tank/ds.ehp leads **Randuin's Omen #1, which is not in his real top-eight at all**, then places Heartsteel #5, Unending Despair #9, Sunfire #11, Hollow Radiance #12, Titanic Hydra #20, Thornmail #23, Overlord's Bloodmail #27. His passive and W give him a health-stacking identity and his damage items scale off HP rather than AD - he buys raw EHP first and HP-to-damage second, and the engine prices only the first half. **A champion with no consensus build is precisely the case a build engine exists to serve, and this one hands him the same 28-champion template it hands everyone else.** Also worth banking as negative evidence: his lethality experiment (Youmuu's 7.01% at 43.26% WR, Voltaic 3.98% at 46.22%, Collector 3.79% at 46.36%) loses 6-7 points of win rate and should be treated as REFUTED, not as a valid alternate route. RM-81: STALE (Q minimum base 40-120 stored vs 30-90 live).
- **Sivir** - GAP RM-86 family (**CORRECTED 2026-07-18 - the original wording of this entry claimed her most-built item did not exist in her pool; that was a top-40 TRUNCATION artifact, not an omission. See the PROBE-DEPTH note below**) - real meta = bottom 98.5%, essentially single-role (23,400-28,000 games, 50.4-51.9% WR), Lethal Tempo 67.14%. Signature = **Essence Reaver (~53.4% of slot-1) -> Navori Flickerblades -> Infinity Edge at 39.57% pick / 56.61% WR**, the most concentrated build in its group; **Navori Flickerblades carries 81.43% any-slot presence**, Infinity Edge 70.24%, Essence Reaver 63.9%. Production carry/ds.dps leads **BotRK #1 and Runaan's Hurricane #2 - neither of which appears in her real core** - and **Navori Flickerblades - her 81.43%-presence most-built item - is ranked #28 of a 111-item carry pool**, with Essence Reaver at **#18** (IE #6, Yun Tal #8, Lord Dominik's #10). The mechanism the engine cannot see: Navori refunds ability cooldowns on crit auto-attacks, so her build buys a crit-to-haste feedback loop rather than raw damage, with Essence Reaver supplying the haste and mana to spam W into it and IE cashing the crit out. A sustained-DPS objective scoring raw auto-attack throughput has no term for cooldown refund. Trap-B artifacts quarantined and worth reusing as a teaching pair: Essence Reaver reads **49.92%**, BELOW her champion average, while Infinity Edge reads 55.68% - that gap is a slot artifact (ER is slot-1, IE is slot-2/3) and is NOT evidence IE is the better item; Guardian Angel at 64.83% is a 5th/6th-slot survivorship number and is meaningless. **Trap A caught live here too:** one source renders Lethal Tempo at 96% / 22,662 games against a 23,423 total - that is the Precision TREE aggregate, not the rune. Do not ingest 96%. RM-81: CURRENT.
- **Skarner** - GAP RM-91 + RM-87 (intra-pool weighting) - real meta = **jungle 65.4% / support 22.4% / top 11.4%**; correcting the batch brief, support is his #2 role, not top. **Thinnest sample in the batch at 6,000-7,600 games**, ~1.1% pick, 49.5-51.1% WR, so this verdict is recorded at MODERATE confidence and his support and top variants are explicitly not characterised. Grasp of the Undying 67-74%. Signature = **Heartsteel 73.37% presence, opening all five top core sequences** -> Unending Despair 52.51% -> Spirit Visage 25.06%, with Protoplasm 21.49% and Thornmail 18.82%. Production tank/ds.ehp leads **Randuin's Omen #1 - which is his 10.09%, ninth-most-picked item** - and buries his 73.37% opener at **#5** (Unending Despair #8, Spirit Visage #9, Thornmail #19, Titanic Hydra #24, Bandlepipes #32). That inversion - a 10% item at #1 over a 73% item at #5 - is the RM-87 fingerprint on top of the RM-91 mechanism. He buys HP twice over, once as EHP and once as a damage stat, and Spirit Visage is the coherent third specifically because its healing amplification MULTIPLIES Unending Despair rather than adding flat resists - a cross-item interaction no per-item scorer models. One apparent oddity that is actually correct and should not be flagged as noise: Bandlepipes 8.26% at 57.31% is a cheap utility item whose Fanfare passive triggers on slowed or CC'd enemies, and his kit has near-permanent CC uptime. Negative evidence banked: Hail of Blades at ~9.4% pick and 40-43% WR is a failed AD/on-hit experiment. RM-81: STALE (Q cooldown 13-7 stored vs 8-3 live - a large drift; immaterial to an item-order verdict but relevant to any future cast-frequency claim).
- **Rell** - GAP RM-90 (**COHORT verdict - see the Renata entry and the RM-90 note below; this is not a per-champion defect**) - real meta = tank support, 99.3% support: World Atlas start -> Celestial Opposition (65.5%) or Solstice Sleigh (26.1%) -> **Zeke's Convergence (70.6% pick, 54.1% WR)** -> **Locket (64.1%)** -> **Knight's Vow (28.3%, 59.0% WR)**; Bandlepipes / Protoplasm Harness tails, Aftershock ~89%. Enchanter heal-shield items are under 1% combined (Redemption 0.34%; Ardent / Staff / Echoes / Moonstone do not register). Production tank/ds.ehp returns a top-10 of which **seven are explicitly never-built for her** - Randuin's #1, Jak'Sho #2, Kaenic #4, Heartsteel #5, Force of Nature #6, Dead Man's #10 (research names Thornmail, Frozen Heart, Abyssal, Randuin's, Kaenic, Force of Nature, Dead Man's, Jak'Sho and Heartsteel as 0-3%) - while her real three-item core sits **Zeke's #16 / Locket #22 / Knight's Vow #32** (Bandlepipes #31, Protoplasm #23). **She DOES have a real durability-to-damage coupling** (passive Break the Mold: basic attacks deal bonus magic damage equal to 5% total armor + 5% total MR, plus a resist-steal of 3% per stack to 5 stacks that she absorbs, plus W dismounted granting 15% bonus armor / 15% bonus MR) - the Ornn/Rammus RM-87 mechanism - but it routes through AUTOS ONLY and her damage is negligible, so RM-87 is a footnote here rather than the verdict. Her passive has EMPTY `damage_blocks` (prose only), the RM-81 class - the FOURTH champion in two batches. E is "Full Tilt", not "Attract and Repel" (renamed in the 13.7 rework)
- **Renata Glasc** - GAP RM-90 (**the cohort's extreme case, and it CONFIRMS the earlier RM-84 "HELD as unrepresentable" call with numbers**) - real meta = WARDEN/UTILITY support, ~99% support: quest item -> Ionian Boots (80.7%) -> **Locket (69.5% first purchased legendary)** -> **Imperial Mandate (44.8%)** -> **Shurelya's (27.8%)** or **Bandlepipes (25.5%)**; Redemption 17.4%, Knight's Vow 16.9%, Mikael's 13.7%; Guardian ~75%. Heal-and-shield-power items are near-absent (Ardent 5.6% with a 5-14% source range, Echoes of Helia 1.09%, Staff of Flowing Water 0.79%, Moonstone <0.5%), and Imperial Mandate is an ALLY-damage amplifier, not selfish AP. **Roughly 85-90% of her kit value lands on ALLIES or ENEMIES** - passive Leverage is consumed by allies' damage, W Bailout revives an ally, E shields allies, R berserks enemies; her only self components are E's shared shield and an optional W self-cast. Production enchanter/ds.hps leads Echoes #1 (1.09%), Ardent #2 (5.6%), Staff #3 (0.79%) - all near-zero real pick - with her actual first legendary Locket only #4 and Imperial Mandate #7, while **Shurelya's and Bandlepipes are NOT IN THE POOL AT ALL** (the pool is a closed 9 items). **This is exactly why RM-84 HELD her as unrepresentable rather than flipping her route:** the tank pool would hand her the generic self-durability list instead, so a flip swaps one wrong list for another. That adjudication is now quantified and confirmed correct
- **RM-90 NOTE - the support cohort ships TWO byte-identical build orders across 12 champions, and prior REFUTEs in this class were too lenient.** Verified directly against the shipped artifact `data/daemon_slayer/16.14.1/build_orders_sr.json` (DISPLAY-keyed - the key is `"Renata Glasc"` with a space): **GROUP A is byte-identical for Renata Glasc / Soraka / Janna / Nami / Lulu / Milio / Sona / Seraphine** (Echoes of Helia -> Mercury's Treads -> Ardent Censer -> Staff of Flowing Water -> Redemption -> Moonstone Renewer, with ad_heavy / ap_heavy / balanced also identical to each other), and **GROUP B is byte-identical for Bard / Taric / Rakan / Rell** (Randuin's -> Mercury's Treads -> Warmog's -> Sterak's -> Jak'Sho -> Spirit Visage). Four layers of cause: (1) `ds.hps` ranks a CLOSED 9-item pool (`enchanter_only=True`) that EXCLUDES Zeke's, Bandlepipes, Solstice Sleigh, Celestial Opposition, Shurelya's and Vigilant Wardstone, every one a real support legendary - `hps.py` lines ~894-907 document the consequence itself; (2) within that pool the order is champion-invariant, 11 of 12 enchanters returning an identical 9/9, with Locket 11.73 / Knight's Vow 10.00 / Mikael's 4.17 literally CONSTANT for every champion (Rell is the sole deviation at 7/9, Locket and Staff swapped); (3) the tank route fails from the other side, since a self-EHP objective cannot value ally-aura items; (4) the intended escape hatch RF2 (`prefer_survivability_by_win`) is default-OFF AND its table `survivability_item_credit_enchanter.json` holds exactly ONE champion, **Rakan** - who no longer routes to `ds.hps` after Slice C moved him to tank - so **RF2 currently fires for nobody**. **The uncomfortable consequence: Alistar / Blitzcrank / Braum / Bard / Rakan were all ruled REFUTE on "correct axis, builds no damage, team-aura is a class-level scope limit". That reasoning under-weighted the outcome** - these champions do not lose some aura nuance, they receive a shipped build order omitting their 60-70%-pick first legendary. Rakan was already flagged in batch19 as the weakest refute in the class. **Do not rule another team-aura support REFUTE without first checking its shipped build order against real pick rates.** Filed as its own task; NOT fixed here (a pool widening is RC-2 work and flipping RF2 is operator-gated)
- **Renekton** - GAP (Aatrox RM-39 ability-bruiser family) - **carries the most direct falsification of a scorer output in the sweep: the engine's #1 pick is measurably his WORST item.** Real meta (top ~94%): **Eclipse first (66.5% pick, n=19,311)** -> **Black Cleaver (60.6%)** -> Death's Dance (30.9%) or Sterak's (25.3%), Spear of Shojin 27.5% rotating slot 2-3, Conqueror 68.7%; true lethality is under 3% combined, so discounting Eclipse he is effectively 100% bruiser. **NOTE for future verdicts: Eclipse lost lethality in V14.1 and is now 60 AD + 15 ability haste, a BRUISER item - do not read an Eclipse-first build as a lethality build.** Production bruiser/ds.hybrid leads BotRK #1, Trinity #2, Essence Reaver #3, Dusk #4, Heartsteel #5, Stormrazor #6, Iceborn #7, Kraken #8, Liandry's #9 - and his 66.5% signature Eclipse only **#10**, behind nine items he does not build; Black Cleaver is **#30**, Sterak's #34, Death's Dance #42, Stridebreaker #44, Hexplate #46, Spear of Shojin #52. **The killer datapoint: BotRK is not merely never-built, it registers 4.2% pick at 48.0% win rate - the ONLY sub-50% item on him.** So the engine's #1 recommendation is, with real outcome data behind it, the worst purchase anyone actually makes on him. **Why:** W Ruthless Predator applies on-hit on every strike and its FIRST strike can crit, so the hooks exist - but W explicitly does NOT scale with attack speed, its extra strikes do not proc per-attack effects, and his damage is ability-dominant (Q up to 140% bonus AD, W 150-225% total AD, E 180-315% bonus AD plus 25-35% armor shred, R 150% bonus AD over 15s) with autos mostly feeding Fury at 5 each. NO durability-to-damage coupling - R Dominus grants 300/500/700 bonus max health but scales off bonus AD and AP only, so health is pure EHP for him
- **Rengar** - GAP (Aatrox RM-39 family, on the assassin scorer) - **fourth independent corroboration that an RM-86 L1 conversion vector must be CONTINUOUS.** Real meta (jungle 88.9%): **Umbral Glaive first (73.3% presence)** -> Ionian Boots (67%) -> **Profane Hydra (70.9%)** -> Lord Dominik's (50.1%) -> Edge of Night (32.7%); Serpent's Fang 17.0%, Hubris 14.2%, Infinity Edge 19.8% at 60.5% WR bought 4th/5th vs tanky comps; Conqueror ~57% total. Split ~75-80% pure lethality, ~20-26% lethality with a late crit splash, **~0% on-hit**. Production assassin/ds.burst leads **BotRK #1 (~0% real)**, Essence Reaver #2, **Trinity #3 (~0%)**, Sundered Sky #4 (0.39%), Eclipse #5 (0.51%), Infinity Edge #6, Terminus #7, Lord Dominik's #8, Mortal Reminder #9, Serylda's #10 (1.4%) - while his two core items sit **Umbral Glaive #13** and **Profane Hydra #38** (Collector #14, Youmuu's #19, Hubris #20, Edge of Night #26, Voltaic #27). **Why:** Q Savagery IS an empowered auto that resets the attack timer, applies on-hit and lifesteal normally, and CAN crit - the hooks are real - but **max Ferocity is 4, not 5** (1 per ability cast), so the empowered Q arrives roughly once per four casts and real burst is 2-3 attacks inside R rather than sustained uptime, converting per-auto stats at a fraction of face value. **CORRECTION BANKED HERE - Opportunity was REMOVED from Summoner's Rift in patch 26.09** (overlapping with Youmuu's); in `items.json` id 6701 carries `inStore: false` and `gold.purchasable: false`, while the map-30 alias 226701 is purchasable on Arena only. Batch19 briefly filed the engine's exclusion of it as a pool-membership defect and **that was WRONG - the candidate filter is correct.** Do not re-file it; an item absent from every ranked pool is not evidence of a bug until `purchasable` / `inStore` and live-game existence are checked
- **Riven** - GAP (Aatrox RM-39 family) - **the STRONGEST single statement of RC-1 in the sweep, because here the mechanic the sim credits is entirely real and entirely unbuilt.** Real meta (top ~68-72%, mid ~26-28%): **Axiom Arc first (43.5% core-slot, 53.1% WR)** -> Ionian Boots (45.9%) -> **Endless Hunger (49.1%)** -> **Death's Dance (59.1%)** -> Serylda's (9.7%); Sundered Sky 28.4%, Black Cleaver 19.7%, Maw 19.4% vs AP; Conqueror ~98.8% of Precision pages. Exactly ONE lethality slot, everything after is bruiser sustain. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid returns a **top-10 that is entirely never-built** - BotRK #1, **Trinity Force #2**, Essence Reaver #3, Heartsteel #4, Stormrazor #5, Eclipse #6, Kraken #7, Dusk #8, Infinity Edge #9, Liandry's #10 - while her real core is buried at Sundered Sky #16, Serylda's #30, Black Cleaver #32, **Axiom Arc #34**, **Death's Dance #40**, **Endless Hunger #41**. **Why this is the sharpest case:** her passive Runic Blade charges her basic attacks, and those charged autos genuinely DO apply on-hit effects, ARE affected by critical-strike modifiers, apply lifesteal at 100%, and each ability cast procs Spellblade cleanly - so every mechanical precondition the sim uses to justify BotRK / Kraken / Infinity Edge / Trinity Force is TRUE, and players build none of them. The research named Trinity Force explicitly as "the notable trap - guide prose claims Runic Blade synergy, real play rate is nil". The reason is CADENCE, not mechanics: Q resets the attack timer so her rate is bound by Q cooldown plus animation-cancelling, ability haste is the real throughput stat, and zero attack-speed items appear in her data. R Wind Slash is a 0-200% missing-health execute a sustained-DPS objective structurally cannot represent. **Riven is the cleanest disproof of "if the kit has the term, credit the term"** - pair her with Rengar (Ferocity 4), Rek'Sai (3 autos per Fury cycle), Pantheon (~1/5 cadence) and Olaf (free kit attack speed)
- **METHOD NOTE (batch19, 2026-07-18) - the invariance measurement, done properly: 32 champions, 4 scorers, forced routes.** batch18 measured this on 3-4 champions; batch19 ran a full matrix (8 champions per scorer, 28 pairs each, top-8 SET overlap and POSITIONAL match, empty build, L16, identical target profile). Result, worst-invariance last: **`ds.ability` 8.00/8 set (6.50/8 positional) - PERFECTLY invariant in set terms across Orianna / Syndra / Veigar / Lux / Xerath / Vex / Zoe / Ziggs, all eight receiving the same eight items**; **`ds.ehp` 7.57/8 set (7.07/8 positional)** across Ornn / Poppy / Rakan / Rammus / Malphite / Leona / Sion / Zac, the only deviation being a Spirit Visage vs Unending Despair swap at slot 8; **`ds.hybrid` 6.04/8 set (2.00/8 positional)**; **`ds.dps` 4.54/8 set (1.89/8 positional)** - the least invariant, exactly as RM-86 section 2 found at top-5. This REPLICATES the RM-86 ordering (ability > ehp > hybrid > dps) independently, at top-8, on a panel four times larger. Two refinements: (1) `ds.dps` does not vary continuously, it splits into TWO CLUSTERS - an on-hit-led group (Quinn / Vayne / Sivir: BotRK, Runaan's, Kraken) and a lethality-crit-led group (Jinx / Caitlyn / Draven: Lord Dominik's, Collector, Infinity Edge, Youmuu's) - so a champion's verdict depends on which cluster its BASE STATS drop it into, which is how Quinn ends up served an on-hit list; (2) the practical consequence is a standing rule, now repeated from batch18 because batch19 strengthened it: **adjudicate every ds.ability, ds.ehp and ds.hybrid verdict on whether the SHARED list happens to fit the champion, never on the list being champion-specific - for `ds.ability` it is provably not.**
- **Rammus** - GAP RM-87 (**the CANONICAL instance of the shape; Ornn got the number first but is the milder case**) - real meta = pure tank, jungle ~89%: **Thornmail 86-92% pick, effectively mandatory rather than a preference** -> Plated Steelcaps (57%) / Mercury's (33%) -> Sunfire Aegis (42%) or Hollow Radiance (27%) -> Jak'Sho (37%) -> Dead Man's (20%) / Randuin's (16%), Aftershock ~71%. Production tank/ds.ehp is close to rank-INVERTED at the extremes: Randuin's #1, **Warmog's Armor #2 (0.35% real pick)**, Jak'Sho #3, Kaenic #4, **Heartsteel #5 (0.85%)**, Force of Nature #6, **Spirit Visage #9 (0.33%)**, Dead Man's #10, Sunfire #11, Hollow Radiance #12, **Thornmail #20**, Frozen Heart #21. Three items totalling **under 1.6% COMBINED pick occupy #2/#5/#9** while his 92%-pick mandatory item sits #20. **MEASURED mechanism:** passive Spiked Shell grants bonus AD equal to **15% TOTAL armor + 15% TOTAL magic resist** (symmetric since the 25.12 rework); W Defensive Ball Curl grants (+30-60% total armor) bonus armor and reflects 15 (+10% total armor +10% total MR) per basic attack received. With W rank 5, armor A becomes 1.6A+47, so passive AD = 0.24A+7 - about **+24 AD per 100 armor** - while **bonus HEALTH contributes ZERO to every damage source in his kit**, and Q/E/R carry no armor ratio at all (they scale AP he never buys). A self-EHP objective weights armor and health by survivability contribution alone, and EHP math rewards health at high resist values, so it pushes toward exactly the health-dense items real players avoid. **TWO structural notes that generalise beyond him:** (1) Spiked Shell has **EMPTY `damage_blocks`** (`parse_status: no_damage`) - the entire armor-to-AD conversion exists only in `effects_descriptions` prose, so RM-87 here is the INTERSECTION of a scorer defect and the RM-81 empty-blocks false-negative class, and **a pure scorer fix cannot reach him because the data layer does not carry the coupling**; (2) `kit_damage_axis` returns **ap** (Q/E/R are MAGIC with AP ratios he never buys) - the THIRD champion where the axis resolver reads the printed ratio rather than the real build (Ornn, Nunu). ORNN CONTRAST: their ds.ehp ranks are near-IDENTICAL (Warmog's #2, Heartsteel #5, Sunfire #11, Thornmail #20 for BOTH) because ds.ehp is invariant - but Ornn's real Thornmail is 38.8% and Rammus's is 92%, so the same engine error costs Rammus far more
- **Quinn** - GAP RM-89 (**the first champion in the sweep to carry BOTH RM-86 root causes at once, which makes her the decisive test case for the L1 plan**) - real meta = **lethality assassin**, and the role has FLIPPED: jungle ~55% / top ~32% / mid ~10%, bot lane dead at ~1.4% (16.10-16.11 added Harrier bonus monster damage, now 75, and 16.14 cut the Harrier cooldown explicitly to open the jungle). Signature **Hubris** most-picked (~22k games, roughly 2x the next), **Profane Hydra** second (~12k); core Profane Hydra or Hubris -> boots -> The Collector -> Edge of Night -> Lord Dominik's -> Infinity Edge 5th/6th, Electrocute ~72%. Real builds split ~85-90% lethality burst, ~10% late lethality-crit hybrid, **~0% on-hit**. **RC-1:** production carry/ds.dps leads **BotRK #1, Runaan's Hurricane #2, Kraken #3, Stormrazor #4** (Terminus #6, Guinsoo's #9) - every one 0-2% real pick - while her lethality core sits Collector #12, Voltaic #19, Youmuu's #23, Edge of Night #26, Serylda's #27, Axiom Arc #28, Hubris #29. **No reroute rescues her - assassin, onhit AND bruiser all still lead BotRK #1.** The mechanism is unusually clean: **Harrier does NOT crit** (crit chance is a COOLDOWN SCALAR only, 5s down to 1.83s) and **attack speed does not raise its proc rate at all**, because the mark is on a static cooldown - so every attack-speed and on-hit item is dead on her, while the sim reads an on-hit-classified empowered attack and prices it as attack-speed throughput. Her passive also has EMPTY `damage_blocks` (prose only, RM-81 class), so the proc's 15-132 + 40% bonus AD is not in the data either. **RC-2, and this is what matters for the fix plan:** her default carry pool holds only **111** items and **structurally EXCLUDES Profane Hydra and Umbral Glaive**, both of which ARE present in the 144-item assassin / onhit / bruiser pools - so her #2 signature item is not in the set being ranked at all. **An L1 conversion gate could suppress BotRK/Runaan's/Kraken and STILL never surface Profane Hydra.** She is concrete proof that L1 and the RC-2 pool work are BOTH required, which RM-86 section 5 anticipated when it warned L1 makes RC-2 slightly worse. SEPARATE DEFECT found via her and filed as its own task: the legendary **Opportunity** (id 6701, 2700g) is in the item catalog but enters **ZERO** ranked pools across 7 champions and 2 archetypes, so it can never be recommended to anyone. Data-currency: Harrier was reworked in season 2026 (15-132 + 40% BONUS AD, previously 10-95 + 16-50% TOTAL AD) and stale mirrors still serve the old numbers
- **Pyke** - GAP RM-88 (**NEW SHAPE - a throughput objective cannot price a discontinuous execute threshold plus a gold-and-reset economy**) - real meta = lethality assassin, support ~98.5%: quest item -> Boots of Swiftness (~75%) -> **Umbral Glaive** -> **Youmuu's Ghostblade** -> **Edge of Night** (that exact 3-core is the top set at 16.1% pick / 52.5% WR), rotations Voltaic Cyclosword / Axiom Arc / Hubris / Serylda's, Hail of Blades ~61%. ZERO crit, attack speed, on-hit or AP in the build. Production assassin/ds.burst leads **BotRK #1 (never-built)**, Sundered Sky #2, Essence Reaver #3, **Trinity Force #4 (never-built)**, Serylda's #5, Lord Dominik's #6, **Infinity Edge #7 (never-built)** - and his real 3-core sits mid-pack at Umbral **#12**, Axiom Arc #14, Youmuu's **#15**, Hubris #16, Collector #18, Edge of Night **#19**, Voltaic #21. MODERATE severity: unlike Olaf or Rek'Sai his core does surface, but the lead is a never-built item. **ROUTE IS CORRECT - do not re-open:** he routes `(assassin, enchanter)` despite a Support-first DDragon tag because `axis_correct_archetype` catches the AD-kit vs AP-enchanter conflict, live-confirming the RM-84 note that Pyke was never broken. **Why:** lethality QUADRUPLE-dips for him - damage, E stun duration, W move speed, R execute threshold AND passive grey-health rate - and the engine models only the damage dip. R Death from Below's threshold is 250-550 (by level) + 80% bonus AD + **1.5 per 1 lethality**, and an execution grants full kill gold to the assisting ally plus a free recast for 20s. That value is a discontinuous step at an HP boundary plus an economy, not throughput, so ANY DPS or burst-throughput objective systematically under-values lethality on him. **CORRECTION to the obvious hypothesis, recorded so nobody re-derives it:** his passive does convert bonus health to AD (1 bonus AD per 14 bonus health, and his max health cannot otherwise increase) - but players do NOT buy health as damage, because at ~37 gold per 14 HP versus ~35 gold per raw AD it is gold-neutral while giving zero durability, hence strictly dominated. The load-bearing coupling runs the OTHER way: the grey-health cap is 80 + 800% bonus AD (ceiling 55% max HP), so buying AD buys effective HP. Health on items he already wants (Edge of Night's 250 HP = ~18 AD) is a bonus, not a build driver. The passive has EMPTY `damage_blocks` - prose only, the RM-81 class again
- **Rek'Sai** - GAP (the Aatrox RM-39 ability-bruiser family; **ties Olaf as the most extreme instance measured**) - real meta = AD bruiser jungler, jungle 88.4%: Stridebreaker (54% first) -> **Spear of Shojin (67.1% overall, her highest-pick item, usually slot 2)** -> Sterak's or Death's Dance -> Guardian Angel; Titanic Hydra 39.3%, Conqueror ~69.5%; lethality is under ~5% combined. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid returns a top-9 that is **entirely never-built** - BotRK #1, Trinity #2, Heartsteel #3, Essence Reaver #4, Kraken #5, Stormrazor #6, Dusk and Dawn #7, Eclipse #8, Liandry's #9, all 0-3% and none appearing in any aggregate core table - while her real core is buried at Sundered Sky #17, Titanic Hydra #23, Black Cleaver #28, Sterak's #31, **Stridebreaker #32**, Death's Dance #39, **Spear of Shojin #48**, Guardian Angel #49. **Why - and this is the THIRD independent corroboration of the RM-86 L1 continuous-conversion argument:** her unburrowed Q Queen's Wrath GENUINELY applies on-hit AND crits on the primary target, and she GENUINELY wants attack speed (autos fill Fury at +25 per on-hit), so the engine is not hallucinating a mechanic - the mechanic is real and STILL does not justify the items, because Q is only 3 empowered autos per Fury cycle, so on-hit and crit convert at a fraction of face value. The meta research named the trap independently: Guinsoo's and Kraken bait the on-hit-Q reading, Infinity Edge and Trinity bait the crit-on-Q reading. Un-modelled: the Fury fill/decay cycle, the burrow/unburrow form swap (two ability sets, range -50 burrowed), E Furious Bite's max-Fury conversion to TRUE damage at 120%, R Void Rush's Marked-as-Prey gating plus its 15-25% max-HP term, and the burrowed Fury-to-heal conversion (9-21.3% max HP by level). **Pair her with Pantheon (~1/5 conversion) and Olaf (free kit attack speed) as the three champions proving a BOOLEAN has-an-AS-term gate is the wrong shape for L1** - all three HAVE the term and none may be credited at face value
- **Rakan** - REFUTE (the Alistar / Blitzcrank / **Braum** team-aura support class) - **and the first post-hoc validation of a Slice C route override.** The RM-84 fix moved him enchanter -> tank via git-tracked `core/ds_support_route_overrides.{json,py}`; independent research confirms it, putting his durability items at ~167% combined across slots (**Zeke's Convergence 69.6%, Locket 64.2%**, Knight's Vow 19.2%, Bandlepipes 14.2%) against ~16% for the entire heal/shield-power shelf (Mikael's 8.4%, Redemption 6.1%, Ardent Censer 0.45%, Moonstone 0.41%, Echoes of Helia 0.32%, Staff of Flowing Water unlisted) - roughly **10:1 toward tank**. An enchanter route would have handed him a list he buys under 1% of the time. Real meta (support 98.9%): Celestial Opposition (65.4%) or Solstice Sleigh (28.6%) -> Ionian Boots (63.9%) -> Zeke's -> Locket -> Knight's Vow, Guardian keystone; he buys essentially no damage item (Imperial Mandate 5.85% is the only one above 3%). Production tank/ds.ehp leads generic self-durability (Randuin's #1, Warmog's #2, Jak'Sho #3, Kaenic #4, Heartsteel #5) and buries his real aura core at **Zeke's #16, Locket #23, Bandlepipes #35, Knight's Vow #36, Shurelya's #87**, with never-built Dead Man's Plate at #10. REFUTE because the AXIS is right and he builds zero damage - the aura/team-EHP mismatch is the CLASS-LEVEL scope limit already logged for Braum in these exact terms (a self-EHP scorer over-ranks Warmog's/Randuin's/Jak'Sho against a warden/aura core of Knight's Vow/Locket), not a per-champ gap. **HONEST CAVEAT, flagged not re-opened: this is the WEAKEST refute in the class** - his two real first legendaries are #16 and #23, deeper than Braum's or Blitzcrank's. If the team-aura scope limit (RM-86 section 5, SETTLED) is ever lifted, Rakan is the champion who gains most; use him as its test case
- **METHOD NOTE (batch18, 2026-07-18) - the ds.ehp and ds.hybrid lists are champion-INVARIANT, and batch18 measured the consequence both ways.** Ornn and Poppy both route tank/ds.ehp and return the SAME top-6 in the SAME order (Randuin's / Warmog's / Jak'Sho / Kaenic Rookern / Heartsteel / Force of Nature; deltas within 1%). That invariance is CORRECT for Poppy (her real core occupies #6/#8/#9/#10) and a DEFECT for Ornn (his signature first item Sunfire Aegis is #11 and his near-tie first Thornmail is #20, under four sub-7%-pick items). Same measurement on ds.hybrid: Ornn / Pantheon / Poppy rerouted to bruiser return an IDENTICAL top-8 item SET (BotRK / Trinity / Heartsteel / Essence Reaver / Dusk and Dawn / Iceborn / Liandry's / Stormrazor), Ornn and Poppy identical 8-of-8 in order, Pantheon with slots 6-7 swapped - across a tank, a tank/fighter and an assassin/fighter. **Olaf is the control that proves the mechanism:** he is also bruiser-routed but overlaps only 4-of-8, because his base AD/AS differ. That is RC-1 stated as a measurement - the champion enters the objective through BASE STATS ONLY, so champions with similar base stats get literally the same build. Extends the `ds.hps` invariance finding (batch16) and the RM-86 section-2 invariance table from top-5 to top-8. **Adjudicate ds.ehp and ds.hybrid verdicts on whether the SHARED list happens to fit the champion, never on the list being champion-specific - it is not.**
- **Olaf** - GAP (the Aatrox RM-39 / Ambessa RM-43 / Darius RM-52 / Hecarim RM-62 ability-bruiser family - the MOST EXTREME instance measured so far) - real meta = AD extended-duel juggernaut (top ~81%): signature first Stridebreaker (~55-59% first-legendary share, 52-59% WR) -> Experimental Hexplate -> Death's Dance -> Sterak's Gage, Plated Steelcaps, Conqueror; Ravenous Hydra ~36% rotates for Stridebreaker. ZERO crit, ZERO on-hit, ZERO lethality, ZERO AP ratios anywhere in the kit. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid returns a top-6 in which **every single item is one he essentially never builds**: Blade of the Ruined King #1 (<2%), Trinity Force #2 (<2%), Essence Reaver #3, Kraken Slayer #4 (~0%), Stormrazor #5, Terminus #6 (Infinity Edge #11 at 2.6%, Heartsteel #9 at ~0%, Liandry's #20 at 0% on a champion with no AP ratio at all). His ENTIRE real core is buried in the #19-#43 band: Sundered Sky #19, Sterak's #29, **Stridebreaker #33**, Death's Dance #40, Experimental Hexplate #41, Ravenous Hydra #43. No reroute rescues him - carry/ds.dps leads BotRK #1, onhit/ds.onhit leads BotRK #1 and floats Liandry's #4 onto a zero-AP champion, tank/ds.ehp gives pure durability. WHY he is worse than Aatrox/Darius rather than the same: those buried a signature at ~#47-51 out of a much larger pool but still surfaced SOME real item high; Olaf surfaces none in the top 12. NUANCE that keeps him from being a pure on-hit-blindness case: his W grants 50-90% attack speed and his passive up to 70% missing-HP attack speed, so the kit genuinely DOES scale with AS - the defect is that the AS arrives FREE from the kit, so purchased AS has sharply diminishing real value, while Q (100% bonus AD, no on-hit, no crit) and E (TRUE damage, 50% AD, cooldown cut by autos) carry the damage and want raw AD + sustain. E's true damage is armor-agnostic and Q self-shreds 20% armor, so the sim's armor-pen and on-hit valuations are both mis-aimed
- **Pantheon** - GAP (the Aatrox RM-39 ability-bruiser family; carries the CLEANEST MECHANISTIC statement of RM-86 found so far) - real meta = AD ability-driven burst-bruiser, role-split support ~42-46% / top ~28-31% / mid ~9-11%: signature first Black Cleaver top (~55% pick, 51.3-52.8% WR, n=16.9k), Eclipse mid, Sundered Sky support (~54.8% WR); then Plated Steelcaps -> Sundered Sky -> Death's Dance or Spear of Shojin, Electrocute. Kraken Slayer / Infinity Edge / Heartsteel / Liandry's are ABSENT from his item tables entirely; BotRK trickles ~6% top and is off-core. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid leads **Blade of the Ruined King #1, Trinity Force #2, Heartsteel #3 (absent from his tables), Essence Reaver #4, Dusk and Dawn #5, Liandry's Torment #6 (absent)** and buries his #1 real item **Black Cleaver at #29** (Youmuu's #41, Death's Dance #38; Eclipse #10 / Sundered Sky #12 / Voltaic Cyclosword #13 do surface mid-pack). No reroute helps: assassin/ds.burst leads BotRK #1 + Lich Bane #4 + Rabadon's #6 (AP items on a pure-AD champion - the RM-41 pollution shape), carry/ds.dps leads BotRK #1. **The mechanism is measurable and is exactly RC-1:** only the empowered W Shield Vault can crit or apply on-hit, and the passive Mortal Will empowers roughly one ability per 5-cast cycle - so attack-speed, crit and on-hit convert at a small FRACTION of face value while the sim reads them at face value. Q Comet Spear cannot crit, applies no on-hit and procs no Sheen; E Aegis Assault triggers Spellblade on CAST; R is pure ability. SHEEN NUANCE (the Illaoi RM-64 shape): Trinity #2 is not hard-invalid because E does proc Spellblade - but his REAL Sheen item is Sundered Sky, which sits #12, so the engine picks the wrong member of a family it is right to consider. Un-modelled: the sub-20%-HP Q execute bonus, the 5-stack cadence, E's directional damage-block, global R pressure
- **Orianna** - GAP (the Ahri RM-40 / Annie RM-45 / Aurora RM-47 / Elise RM-54 / Gragas RM-59 burst-mage family, SIXTH champion) - real meta = AP BURST control mage, mid 94.8%: signature first Luden's Echo (~55% pick any-slot, ~67% as first completed legendary, n=9,079) -> Sorcerer's Shoes -> Shadowflame -> Zhonya's Hourglass -> Rabadon's Deathcap, Hextech Rocketbelt rotating slot 3, Summon Aery. NOTHING in her kit ticks - Q/W/E/R are all discrete front-loaded hits, no DoT, no %maxHP, no execute. Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's Torment #1 at a 3.3% real pick rate** and buries her signature **Luden's Echo #13**; her AP amp core DOES surface (Rabadon's #4, Shadowflame #5, Zhonya's #12), so the AXIS is right and only the LEAD is wrong. assassin/ds.burst reroute REFUTED - it leads Void Staff #1 / Lich Bane #2 but floats **Blade of the Ruined King #4** onto a 100%-AP champion (the RM-41 AD-pollution), so the fix STAYS in the mage scorer, exactly like Ahri / Annie / Aurora / Elise / Gragas. **IMPORTANT REFINEMENT to the whole family - the standing "Liandry's + Blackfire lead" is TWO findings, not one.** Blackfire Torch at #2 is NOT a defect for her: it is genuinely meta at ~31% pick and actually OUT-WINS Luden's (47.6-48.1% vs 46.0%). Players buy it for AP + ability haste + mana, not for the burn her kit cannot apply - so the engine is right about Blackfire for the wrong reason, and wrong about Liandry's outright. Future family verdicts should score the two items separately. Data note: her R change (95% -> 110% AP) landed upstream two patches before 16.14, so the 16.14 re-extract brought RC current rather than introducing a new value; no probe here is stale on it
- **Ornn** - GAP RM-87 (**NEW SHAPE - the first ds.ehp gap that is an intra-pool WEIGHTING defect rather than an omitted off-axis core**) - real meta = pure tank, top 95.3%: signature first Sunfire Aegis (~32-37% build share, 51-56% WR, n~21k) with Thornmail a near-tie alternative (38.8% pick / 58.9% WR) -> Jak'Sho -> Kaenic Rookern or Unending Despair, Plated Steelcaps 66.6%, Grasp 85.8%. He builds **ZERO damage items**, so the tank/ds.ehp route is the CORRECT axis and this is NOT the Amumu RM-44 / Cho'Gath RM-51 / Galio RM-55 shape (no separate AP core is being omitted - there is none to omit). The defect is entirely INSIDE the durability pool: production ds.ehp leads Randuin's #1, **Warmog's Armor #2 (1.9% real pick)**, Jak'Sho #3, Kaenic #4, **Heartsteel #5 (6.7%)**, Force of Nature #6, **Spirit Visage #8 (1.0%)**, **Dead Man's Plate #10 (1.7%)** - four sub-7% items ranked ABOVE his signature **Sunfire Aegis #11**, with **Thornmail #20** and Abyssal Mask #14 (2.2%) and Iceborn Gauntlet #18 (0.8%) also mis-placed. His real 2nd/3rd DO surface (Jak'Sho #3, Kaenic #4, Unending Despair #9), so this is a mis-ORDER not a miss. **MEASURED ROOT CAUSE - resists pay twice for Ornn and the scorer counts them once.** E Searing Charge deals 80-260 **+ 40% bonus armor + 40% bonus magic resist**, a direct durability-to-damage coupling, and passive Living Forge grants +10% bonus armor/MR/health rising to +30% per legendary upgraded, which inflates the same resist pool E scales off. A self-EHP objective weights raw health and resists by their survivability contribution alone, so it systematically under-ranks resist-dense items (Thornmail, Frozen Heart, Sunfire) against health-dense ones (Warmog's, Heartsteel) for him specifically. AXIS SUB-NOTE: `kit_damage_axis` returns **ap** for Ornn while the route is tank (axis-NEUTRAL) - structurally the Nunu batch17 shape - but UNLIKE Nunu it is HARMLESS here, because Nunu's real build wants a Liandry's the tank pool cannot reach and Ornn's real build wants no AP item at all. Do not fold Ornn into the Nunu route call. Team-side value (Living Forge upgrading ALLY legendaries, up to ~5000g of free team stats; Brittle amplifying ALLY damage) is the SETTLED team-aura scope limit from RM-86 section 5, not re-opened here
- **Poppy** - REFUTE (the Dr. Mundo / Alistar / Blitzcrank / Braum durability-served class; the decisive CONTRAST CONTROL to Ornn RM-87) - real meta = tank in all three roles, support 56.6% / top 26.0% / jungle 16.7%: top and jungle open Sundered Sky (~60% / 56.5% WR) then pure durability - Winter's Approach/Fimbulwinter -> Spirit Visage (61.3%) or Unending Despair (60.9%), Thornmail / Jak'Sho / Force of Nature / Randuin's rotating; support runs Celestial Opposition (~98%) -> Dead Man's Plate -> Locket. Production tank/ds.ehp serves her: Force of Nature #6, Spirit Visage #8, Unending Despair #9, Dead Man's Plate #10, Jak'Sho #3, Kaenic #4, Randuin's #1 and Thornmail #21 are all real items in her rotation, and the never-built items are correctly SUPPRESSED - Blade of the Ruined King #73, Liandry's #50, Trinity Force #47. **The decisive kit fact: no Poppy ability converts her OWN bonus HP or resists into damage.** Q Hammer Shock reads the TARGET's max HP (9% per hit), E and R scale with bonus AD, the passive shield reads her own max HP into EHP only, and W's +12-24% total armor/MR is a multiplicative resist amp that is again EHP-only. So there is no durability-to-damage coupling to model and a pure EHP objective is the right objective - the EXACT inverse of Ornn, whose E scales off 40% bonus armor + 40% bonus MR. TWO class-level scope limits, NEITHER a per-champ gap: (a) Heartsteel #5 is never-built for her - the shared self-EHP pool over-rank already logged for Mundo/Alistar/Braum; (b) her signature FIRST item **Sundered Sky sits #24** because it is an AD/heal item a self-EHP scorer structurally cannot value - the same limit as Mundo's buried Titanic Hydra. She FAILS the GAP bar on all three counts (axis correct, real core surfaces top-10, no omitted off-axis core), so REFUTE despite the #24 signature. Her W dash-block and R displacement are near-total utility and are the main reason she is picked - invisible to a DPS+EHP sim, and the same team-side scope limit as Ornn's
- **METHOD CORRECTION (batch16, 2026-07-18) - the standing `item_ids=[]` probe under-ranks AMP-ONLY items.** An amp multiplies throughput that an empty build does not yet have, so amp items score ~0 from empty and climb steeply with depth. Measured on Nami: Moonstone Renewer 0.4 (empty) -> 11.0 (+Echoes) -> 12.9 (+Ardent) -> 14.9 (+Staff), moving 9th -> 2nd; Redemption moves 6th -> 1st. The empty-build order is therefore NOT the engine's opinion about a finished build. **Re-probe at depth before banking any verdict that turns on an amp, conditional, or proc item.** This does NOT invalidate rank-order trust generally - the Naafiri verdict below was re-probed at four depths and held identical. Prior enchanter verdicts banked on empty-build probes should be re-checked. Memory: `project_ds_hps_champion_invariant`.

- **SCORER-LEVEL FINDING (batch16) - `ds.hps` item ranking is champion-INVARIANT.** Ten enchanters (Nami, Morgana, Soraka, Janna, Sona, Yuumi, Lulu, Karma, Milio, Seraphine) return the IDENTICAL order at every build depth and target state, deltas within 0.6. Two causes, both grep-confirmed: (1) the pool is a closed 10-entry registry `data/daemon_slayer/<patch>/enchanter_items.json` (9 rankable legendaries) and items outside it "contribute zero"; real-meta items **Shurelya's Battlesong (10.1% on Nami) and Dawncore (2.1%) are not in it at all**; (2) the engine DOES compute per-champion heal/shield throughput (`ability_hps.py:320` parses `raw_modifiers`; Soraka 10.21 heal/s vs Morgana 2.52 shield/s vs Nautilus 6.93 vs Nami 6.59 at L16) but the seam that would let that number weight items, `apply_ability_hsp_amp` (DEFAULT-OFF, ENGINE 1.202.0), is a parameter of `compute_hps` ONLY - it is absent from `rank_items_by_hps`'s signature and from `rank_for_primary_archetype`, so it cannot influence ranking at any call depth. **Consequence: an enchanter verdict must be adjudicated on the ARCHETYPE ROUTE, not the item order** - the order carries no champion-specific information. Same shape as the open Vayne / Silver Bolts question: when a scorer cannot represent a kit, that is a finding about the scorer.

- **SCORER-LEVEL FINDING (batch17, 2026-07-18) - the batch16 `ds.hps` invariance is NOT an enchanter quirk; it is the engine-wide shape.** Measured across six scorers, 6 champions each, same target state (armor 100 / MR 60 / maxHP 2500 / bonusHP 1200) at L16: mean top-5 set overlap is **`ds.ehp` 5.0/5, `ds.ability` 5.0/5, `ds.hps` 5.0/5, `ds.hybrid` 4.5/5, `ds.burst` 2.8/5, `ds.dps` 2.2/5**. This **refutes the standing 2026-07-13 read in both directions**: "mages / tanks / enchanters are CLEAN" is false (they are the MOST invariant), and "fault is the AD sustained-DPS path only" is false and inverted (`ds.dps` is the LEAST invariant - it genuinely discriminates crit-vs-on-hit-vs-Essence-Reaver from base stats). Full analysis, the two root causes, and the fix ladder are in **`docs/specs/RM-86_scorer_kit_blindness_investigation.md`**. Practical consequence for this sweep: **four of the five batch17 verdicts below are INSTANCES of RM-86, not independent specs** - do not mint a new RM number for "the scorer returned the archetype template again".

- **THE VAYNE / SILVER BOLTS QUESTION IS CLOSED (batch17)** - it had been carried unanswered across three sessions. `ds.dps` does **not** model Silver Bolts *as stat-independent*; it does not model it **at all**. Static: `agents/daemon_slayer/dps.py` never imports `abilities`/`AbilitiesSnapshot` and the string `damage_blocks` does not appear in the file. Behavioural: sweeping Vayne's target_max_hp 1200 -> 2500 -> 5000 at carry/L16, **only BotRK's delta moves (70 -> 120 -> 217)** because BotRK carries its own `%maxHP` on-hit term; Runaan's (66), Kraken (61) and Stormrazor (52) are flat at all three. If W were modelled, every attack-speed item would scale with target HP. Tank Vayne is therefore unrepresentable regardless of item filters. Do not re-open this probe.

- **Neeko** - **GAP (instance of RM-86)**. **ENGINE:** routes `("mage","enchanter")`, kit_axis `ap` - route is CORRECT. `ds.ability` at tanky_L16 returns Liandry's Torment(37) > Blackfire Torch(19) > Void Staff(11) > Rabadon's(11) > Shadowflame(10) > Mejai's(9), which is byte-comparable to what Morgana, Lux, Syndra, Veigar and Nunu get from the same scorer. **META:** Support 75.5% / Mid 15.3% (aggregator D 9,849 games Em+ 16.14, WR 50.19%). Hextech Rocketbelt is item 1 and Zhonya's item 2 in **every one of the top five core paths**; third slot splits Stormsurge 7.97% / Imperial Mandate 7.84% / Shadowflame 7.47% / Morellonomicon 7.16% / Frozen Heart 4.89%. Boots Ionian 58.6% over Sorcerer's 34.15%. **DISCRIMINATOR: Rocketbelt and Zhonya's - the real #1 and #2 - are both absent from the engine's top 8, and Liandry's/Blackfire (the engine's #1/#2) do not appear in any leading build.** Neeko's kit carries no damage-over-time, so the Liandry's lead is unconvertible-stat credit, exactly RC-1. Zero enchanter items in her real build, so the `enchanter` secondary is correctly demoted. **STALENESS:** 0 findings - clean. **Not in the 16.14 CDragon re-extract delta**, so this verdict is data-current.

- **Nidalee** - **GAP (instance of RM-86)**. **ENGINE:** routes `("mage","assassin")`, kit_axis `ap` - route is CORRECT. `ds.ability` returns Liandry's(46) > Void Staff(31) > Rabadon's(28) > Shadowflame(27) > Blackfire(27) > Hextech Gunblade(24). The `assassin` reroute is WORSE, not better - `ds.burst` hands an AP jungler **BotRK(347) > Essence Reaver(330) > Trinity Force(327) > Infinity Edge(276) > Lich Bane(274)**, an AD template reaching an AP champion because `burst.py:85-88` sources its auto component from `compute_dps`. **META:** Jungle 88.9% (aggregator D 16,680 games Em+, WR 51.88%, 5/77 junglers, S-tier); the AP-mid variant is effectively dead at 1.4%. **Lich Bane is item 1 in 100% of the top five core paths** (Lich Bane + Rocketbelt + Zhonya's 15.23% leads), then Zhonya's / Rabadon's / Mejai's; Sorcerer's 86.17%. **DISCRIMINATOR: Lich Bane is her single most-picked item and sits outside the engine's mage top 8; Nashor's Tooth and Malignance - both textbook-expected - are ABSENT from real builds too**, so this is not "the engine is behind the meta", it is the spellblade/AA-reset axis being unrepresented. **STALENESS:** 0 findings. Not in the re-extract delta.

- **Nilah** - **GAP (RM-86 + a ROUTE gap; the most severe verdict in the batch)**. **ENGINE:** routes `("bruiser","assassin")`, kit_axis `ad`. **The kit-axis table entry is CONFIRMED STILL INERT** - `axis_correct_archetype` compares archetype axis to kit axis, and bruiser is already `ad`, so there is no conflict for it to resolve and it passes her through untouched (the same structural blindness as the Morgana AP-vs-AP case, mirrored onto AD). `ds.hybrid` at tanky_L16 returns BotRK(73) > Trinity Force(41) > **Heartsteel(35)** > Essence Reaver(39) > **Liandry's Torment(34)** > Stormrazor(37). The `carry` reroute does NOT rescue her: BotRK > Trinity > Essence Reaver > Stormrazor > Heartsteel > Kraken > Liandry's. **META:** Bottom 98.8% (aggregator D 7,406 games Em+). Crit/lifesteal ADC, unambiguous - The Collector 67.09% first item, then Infinity Edge; the single leading core is **Collector > IE > Lord Dominik's at 37.24% (61.63% WR)**; Bloodthirster 71.7% and Phantom Dancer 71.33% by inclusion; Gluttonous Greaves 44% (lifesteal boots outpick Berserker's). **DISCRIMINATOR: the engine offers Heartsteel and Liandry's Torment to a crit marksman - a direct anchor violation - while The Collector, Phantom Dancer, Bloodthirster and Lord Dominik's are absent from BOTH the bruiser and carry top-8.** Note this is a DOUBLE finding: the route is wrong AND the correct route also fails, so a route-only fix (the RM-84 mechanism) would NOT close it. **STALENESS:** 1 finding - Q base minimum physical [5,25] vs wiki [0,40]. Not in the re-extract delta.

- **Nocturne** - **GAP (instance of RM-86); route is CORRECT.** **ENGINE:** routes `("bruiser","assassin")`, kit_axis `ad`. `ds.hybrid` returns BotRK(84) > Trinity Force(48) > Heartsteel(37) > Essence Reaver(44) > Stormrazor(41) > Kraken Slayer(41) > Dusk and Dawn(36) > Liandry's(35) - **the same six items Nilah gets, in nearly the same order, on a completely different kit.** **META:** Jungle 97.8% (aggregator D 47,895 games Em+). Genuinely two-archetype: bruiser leads on volume (**Experimental Hexplate + Stridebreaker + Black Cleaver 13.29%, WR 53.45%**) and lethality wins on win rate (Profane Hydra + Axiom Arc + Voltaic Cyclosword 10.92%, WR 57.87%). No crit build exists. Tank boots ~80% combined (Plated 44.73% / Merc's 35.1%), zero Berserker's. **DISCRIMINATOR:** the bruiser ROUTE is meta-confirmed, so this is purely an item-order gap - Experimental Hexplate (his dominant first item, an ultimate-haste item) and Stridebreaker are absent from the engine's top 8, and BotRK, the engine's #1, appears in no leading build. **STALENESS:** 2 findings - Q [65,245] vs wiki [65,225]; Q bonus AD [20,60] vs [15,55]. **CORRECTION TO THE BATCH BRIEF: Nocturne IS in the 16.14 CDragon re-extract delta** (Q `TotalDamage` base [65..290] -> [65..265] at max rank, a 8.6% cut). The brief's "none of the five is in the stale-CDragon 49" referred to the strict-DROP set, which is a different set from the 16.11 -> 16.14 re-extract delta. The verdict is unaffected - the change is a magnitude, not an axis - and it was measured AFTER the re-extract landed, so it is data-current either way.

- **Nunu & Willump** - **GAP (ROUTE, tank -> mage; RM-84-shaped but from a TANK tag, not a Support tag).** **ENGINE:** routes `("tank","mage")` while **`kit_damage_axis("Nunu")` returns `ap`**. `axis_correct_archetype` sees the mismatch and deliberately declines to act, because its contract treats tank as axis-NEUTRAL and only corrects a damage archetype on the opposite axis. So the engine correctly identifies his kit as AP and still routes him to `ds.ehp`, which returns Randuin's Omen(1992) > Warmog's(1757) > Jak'Sho(1633) > Kaenic Rookern(1628) > Heartsteel(1582) > Force of Nature(1339). The `mage` reroute returns **Liandry's Torment(38)** > Blackfire(20) > Rabadon's(13) > Void(12) > Shadowflame(12). **META:** Jungle 81.3-84.9% (aggregator D ~12,000 games Em+). The real build is **AP-FIRST then tank**: leading cores are Rocketbelt + Stormsurge + Shadowflame 3.3% (WR 53.2%) and **Liandry's Torment + Dead Man's Plate + Spirit Visage 2.65%**; slots 3+ are MR tanking (Spirit Visage 909 games, Kaenic Rookern 684). Boots split Plated Steelcaps 33.11% / Sorcerer's 29.86%. Sunfire, Heartsteel and Jak'Sho do NOT lead. **DISCRIMINATOR: Liandry's - the engine's mage-route #1 - is a genuine real-meta core for him, and it is structurally unreachable from the tank pool.** This is the strongest route-only GAP in the batch: unlike Nilah, the reroute target already produces a defensible build. Riftmaker and Blackfire, both textbook-expected, are absent from real builds - so the engine's #2 is still wrong even after the reroute (RM-86 residue). **STALENESS:** 2 findings - Q cooldown [12,8] vs wiki [13,9]; E [16,48] vs [15,45]. Not in the re-extract delta. **NOT auto-flipped this session** - the Slice C roster shipped in this batch is deliberately scoped to Support-tag champions; extending it to axis-neutral TANK-tag misroutes is a separate operator-gated decision with its own blast radius.

- **Morgana** - **GAP RM-84** (archetype MISROUTE, enchanter -> mage; the engine's own mage scorer already produces the correct build, only the route is wrong). **FIXED 2026-07-18** - see the Slice C note at the end of this entry. **ENGINE:** `default_for_champion("Morgana")` returns `("enchanter","mage")`, so the live path scores her on `ds.hps` and returns Echoes(29) > **Ardent Censer(15) > Staff of Flowing Water(12)** > Locket(12) > Knight's Vow(10) > Redemption(9) > Imperial Mandate(6) > Mikael's(4) > Moonstone(0). Her real first item is **structurally unreachable** - Liandry's Torment is not in the 9-item enchanter pool. The `mage` reroute at tanky_L16 returns **Liandry's Torment(38) > Blackfire Torch(20)** > Void Staff > Rabadon's > Shadowflame, which matches real meta including the ~1.8x Liandry's-over-Blackfire ratio. **META:** Support 84.5% / Mid 7.5% / Jungle 5.2% (aggregator D Em+); first legendary **Liandry's 45.74%** (8,747 g, 50.85%) vs Blackfire 25.08% - aggregator C labels the Blackfire variant "Off-Meta". Core Zaz'Zak's > Ionian > Liandry's > Zhonya's (53.04%) > Rylai's (33.55%). Keystone Arcane Comet 73.3%. Damage **88.2% magic**. DISCRIMINATOR: **Ardent Censer and Staff of Flowing Water appear in NO source, at NO build depth, in ANY role** (absent from aggregator A's full ~19,100-game support table complete down to 0.41%, absent from aggregator D' support item universe, absent from the mid and jungle pools); Echoes 0.47%, Moonstone <0.41%. Imperial Mandate 17.51% is the sole crossover and is a mark/damage-amp item procced by Dark Binding's root, NOT heal-power. So the engine's #2 and #3 picks for her have a literal 0.00% real pick rate. **STALENESS:** 1 finding - W "Tormented Shadow" `base:Minimum Damage Per Tick` Meraki [6,28] vs wiki [9,35] (engine under-credits 50% at rank 1 / 25% at rank 5). Riot shipped NO Morgana change in 26.11-26.14 (all four patch lists enumerated), so this drift predates 26.11 and is Meraki-freeze residue. It CONVERGES with the misroute rather than being independent: her W DoT is exactly the axis Liandry's rewards, so the stale under-credit further suppresses the correct build. **ROOT CAUSE (pinned):** DDragon tags are `["Support","Mage"]` and `tag_to_archetype("Support") == "enchanter"`; `axis_correct_archetype` cannot catch it because enchanter and mage are both AP (it only resolves AD/AP conflicts). **20 champions are Support-tag-first** and all route to enchanter: Bard, Ivern, Janna, Lulu, Milio, Morgana, Nami, Pyke, Rakan, Renata Glasc, Senna, Seraphine, Sona, Soraka, Taric, Thresh, Yuumi, Zilean (+2 Renata alias keys). Correct for Nami/Soraka/Janna/Lulu/Milio/Yuumi; wrong for at least Morgana and **Pyke (an AD lethality assassin routed to a heal scorer)**. **FIX PATH:** the `data/cs_archetype_picks.json` override table already exists and is **empty (`{}`)**; this is the mechanism BACKLOG "DS cross-eval Tier-2 nominations (A)" describes, which already names Bard/Morgana/Seraphine/Taric/Thresh/Zilean - this verdict supplies the SR evidence for Morgana (that nomination was ARAM-scoped) and adds Pyke. Operator-gated, NOT auto-flip. **SHIPPED 2026-07-18 (Slice C) - with two corrections to the analysis above.** (1) **Pyke and Senna were never broken.** `default_for_champion("Pyke")` returns `("assassin","enchanter")` and Senna returns `("carry","enchanter")` - `axis_correct_archetype` DOES catch them, because their kits are AD and enchanter is AP, which is exactly the AD-vs-AP conflict it resolves. The Support-tag misroute only survives when the target is AP (Morgana) or axis-neutral (Thresh/Rakan/Taric/Bard). (2) **`data/cs_archetype_picks.json` is the WRONG fix path** - it is GITIGNORED runtime operator state (`.gitignore:77`), so an override written there reaches neither CI, the Share mirror, nor any other machine, and it re-creates the committed-precompute pollution vector whose UI was removed in LEDGER 824. The fix shipped instead as a git-tracked roster (`core/ds_support_route_overrides.{json,py}`) consumed by `default_for_champion`, mirroring the Slice A (`_AP_ASSASSIN_IDS`) and Slice B (`ds_onhit_ap_roster`) precedents. All 18 Support-tag champions were adjudicated per champion against live 16.14 Emerald+ data: **5 overridden** (Morgana -> mage; Thresh / Rakan / Taric / Bard -> tank), **9 confirmed correct** (Nami, Soraka, Janna, Lulu, Milio, Yuumi, Sona, Ivern, Seraphine-support), **2 already correct via axis correction** (Pyke, Senna), and **2 deliberately HELD as unrepresentable** - Renata Glasc (real build splits tank-engage Locket ~50% against utility-mage Imperial Mandate ~40%, with Redemption ~15% genuinely inside the enchanter pool) and Zilean (real build is AP UTILITY - Solstice Sleigh 57.5%, Shurelya's, Imperial Mandate, Cosmic Drive - with Liandry's, Luden's, Malignance and Rylai's ALL absent, so the mage route's Liandry's-first lead would be a FRESH never-build violation). Flipping those two would swap one anchor violation for another, so they are tracked for a future scorer rather than routed. **Aura caveat, not a new defect:** Thresh/Rakan/Taric/Bard really build ALLY-AURA tank items (Locket, Knight's Vow, Zeke's, Solstice Sleigh) and `ds.ehp` values SELF EHP, so it leads with Randuin's/Jak'Sho/Kaenic/Warmog's - the SETTLED team-aura scope limit, the same one logged in the Nautilus and Nami REFUTEs. The override is still a strict improvement under the anchor: it stops the engine offering Echoes of Helia / Ardent Censer / Staff of Flowing Water as the top 3 to champions who buy them at 0.00% at every depth.

- **Naafiri** - **GAP RM-83** (on-hit + crit mis-credit on a kit with no attack-speed ratio and no auto-attack reset; the BACKLOG "(B) champion-kit-aware DPS crediting / B1 melee-applicability gate" class, now with a concrete melee instance). **ENGINE:** `ds.burst` ranks **Blade of the Ruined King #1** at every target state and level (tanky_L11/L16, squishy_L11/L16) and **Infinity Edge #3-4**, while **Voltaic Cyclosword sits #15 (squishy_L16) / #22 (tanky_L16)**. Re-probed at four build depths to rule out the empty-build artifact above - BotRK holds #1 at empty, +Voltaic, +Voltaic+Youmuu's and +Voltaic+Youmuu's+Serylda's, and IE CLIMBS to #2. Structural check: Naafiri is **melee (attack_range 125)** and **every damage block in her kit scales on `bonus_ad_pct` only** - Q/E/R carry zero `total_ad_pct`, zero `ap_pct`, zero attack-speed term. Her damage is ability-borne bonus AD, which lethality and bonus-AD items multiply and which crit/AS items do not touch. **META:** now a JUNGLER 72-77%; **Voltaic Cyclosword 87% first item**; Conqueror 91% jungle; Youmuu's DEAD at 0.36%; **no crit, no on-hit, no AP, no tank at any build depth**; no AS ratio and no AA reset in kit; no changes 26.12-26.14 (last numeric V26.10). **STALENESS:** 2 findings, both OVER-credits, both on her primary damage - Q `base:Minimum Bonus Physical Damage` Meraki [30,90] vs wiki [30,80] (+12.5% at max rank) and **R `base:Physical Damage` Meraki [150,350] vs wiki [150,300] (+16.7% at rank 3)**. So the engine is scoring an inflated ult on top of a mis-credited item axis; the two errors compound rather than cancel.

- **Nami** - **REFUTE** (the engine's core order matches real meta once probed at depth; residuals are the SETTLED team-aura scope limit plus two pool omissions). **ENGINE:** `ds.hps` from empty returns Echoes(29) > Ardent(16) > Staff(13) > Locket(12) > Knight's Vow(10) > Redemption(9) > Imperial(7) > Mikael's(4) > Moonstone(0) - but per the METHOD CORRECTION above that empty-build order buries the amps. Re-probed at depth: after Echoes the order is Ardent > Staff > Redemption > Locket > **Moonstone** > Imperial; after Echoes+Ardent+Staff it is Redemption > **Moonstone(14.9)** > Locket > Imperial. **META:** Support 99.8-100% (there is no mid Nami: 24 games, 37.5% WR); first legendary **Echoes of Helia 39.4%** (16,196 g, 53.4%) > Imperial Mandate 30.4% > Moonstone 14.6%; overall pick Echoes 51.07% / **Moonstone 50.55%** / Mandate 31.37% / Ardent 25.10% / Staff 17.63% / Redemption 14.47%; core Dream Maker (free support-quest upgrade, not a legendary) > Ionian 90.19% > Echoes > Moonstone > Ardent/Redemption; Summon Aery 96.6%. DISCRIMINATOR: **zero mage Nami** - Liandry's, Blackfire, Shadowflame, Stormsurge all ABSENT across 42,295 Em+ games and absent again at Master+ (6,764); the "Liandry's Nami" in search results is an ARAM guide. Engine #1 Echoes matches real #1; Moonstone recovers to #2 at depth, matching real #2. Residual mismatches are Locket (engine #3-4 vs real 2.52%) and Knight's Vow (engine #5 vs real 1.48%) - both team-aura EHP items, i.e. the SETTLED Alistar / Braum / Leona / Maokai personal-EHP-vs-team-aura scope limit, not a new finding - plus **Shurelya's Battlesong (10.14%) and Dawncore (2.12%) missing from the closed pool entirely** (logged in the SCORER-LEVEL FINDING above, not charged to Nami). **STALENESS:** 1 finding, and it is a **patch-note-CONFIRMED true positive**: E "Tidecaller's Blessing" `base:Bonus Magic Damage Per Hit` Meraki [20,60] vs wiki [20,80]. Riot **26.14 buffed Nami E 20/30/40/50/60 => 20/35/50/65/80** (released 2026-07-14). The engine is running the PRE-BUFF value and under-credits her E by 25% at max rank. This validates the RM-81 detector against an independent authority. Does not move the verdict (her build axis is heal/shield throughput, not E damage).

- **Nasus** - **GAP RM-85** (archetype POOL PARTITION: his real build spans the bruiser and tank pools and NEITHER route can express it). **ENGINE:** default is `("bruiser","tank")`, so the live path is `ds.hybrid`. Note `ds.hybrid` emits NO `delta` key at all - it uses `delta_dps` / `delta_ehp` / `hybrid_delta_pct`; a probe reading `delta` silently gets 0.0 for every row (the `reference_ds_client_effective_score_parse` trap; rank ORDER stays valid). Bruiser route: Trinity Force #1 (1.34%) > BotRK #2 > Dusk and Dawn > **Heartsteel #4** > Essence Reaver > Liandry's > Iceborn #7; at depth (+Protoplasm+Iceborn) **Heartsteel #1, BotRK #2**. **Protoplasm Harness, Frozen Heart and Spirit Visage are ABSENT from the bruiser top-40 at EVERY depth probed.** The tank route DOES reach them (Spirit Visage #8, Frozen Heart #20, Protoplasm #23, Iceborn #17, Heartsteel #5) but drops Trinity Force entirely. **META:** Nasus is a **JUNGLER 55.6%** (22,088 g, 51.96%) over top 38.0%; jungle first legendary **Protoplasm Harness 59.98%** (12,176 g, 52.85%) > Trinity 29.67% > Iceborn 8.35%; top first **Trinity 60.06%**; jungle core Protoplasm > Swiftness > **Iceborn (item 2, 51.90%)** > **Spirit Visage (item 3, 44.42%, 57.62% WR)** / **Frozen Heart (item 3, 23.28%, 58.68% WR)**; Fleet Footwork 87.49% jungle; damage 77.6% physical. DISCRIMINATORS: **on-hit ZERO** (BotRK, Terminus, Wit's End, Nashor's absent at every slot 1-6 in BOTH roles, and top item-6 lists down to 1 game); **Heartsteel ZERO** (jungle absent all slots, top item-6 only, 1 game); crit near-zero jungle (Essence Reaver is a top-lane pocket at 15.11% item 1). So the engine leads with two items at ~0% real pick rate (Heartsteel, BotRK) while three items making up the majority of his real build are unreachable from his default route. **CORRECTION to a prior BACKLOG premise:** BACKLOG "(B) R129 bruiser/hybrid XOR" lists Nasus Q among auto-empowers that `compute_dps` already counts as an auto - true, and Meraki + the local snapshot + overlay app E all confirm **Siphoning Strike DOES reset the attack timer** (so the engine's auto-side crediting is defensible). The gap here is NOT the XOR: it is that the bruiser candidate pool excludes the defensive-ability-haste items his real build is built on. **STALENESS:** 1 finding - Q `base:Bonus Physical Damage` Meraki [35,115] vs wiki [40,120] (engine under-credits ~12.5% at rank 1, ~4% at rank 5). Riot shipped no Nasus change in 26.11-26.14, so this is Meraki-freeze residue; it is small and does not move the verdict, which turns on pool membership rather than magnitude.

- **Nautilus** - **REFUTE** (the Leona / Alistar / Braum / Maokai class; the engine's HP-heavy lead is correct for him twice over, and the only mismatch is the settled team-aura scope limit). **ENGINE:** `ds.ehp` returns Randuin's Omen #1 (2184) > Warmog's #2 (1862) > Jak'Sho #3 > Kaenic Rookern #4 > Heartsteel #5 > Force of Nature #6 > Shield of Molten Stone #7 > Spirit Visage #8. Target state does not move it (tanky and squishy identical, by design - `ds.ehp` keys on `enemy_ad_share`/`enemy_ap_share`). **SHARPENING, verified not assumed:** the engine genuinely models his W max-HP shield scaling through the `raw_modifiers` path (`[50,60,70,80,90]` flat + `[8,9,10,11,12]` % maximum health), and his shield throughput rises **+67% from HP items** (6.93 -> 11.56 shield/s adding Warmog's + Heartsteel), while Morgana's AP-scaling shield correctly stays flat under the same items. So the HP lead is right for personal EHP AND right for his kit's HP-to-shield conversion - the same "sharpening in the engine's favour" noted for Maokai. **META:** support 97.1%, Leona/Alistar/Braum class; **ZERO AP items at any build depth** ("AP Nautilus" returns "No data found" on aggregator B) and the engine surfaces none - correct; W shield scales MAX HP (8-12%), not bonus HP; nothing in his kit scales with armor, MR or bonus HP; Aftershock 87.2%. Aura/utility items are buried (Zeke's #17, Thornmail #20, Frozen Heart #21, Locket #25, Knight's Vow #35), which is the SETTLED personal-EHP-vs-team-aura scope limit already adjudicated for Alistar / Braum / Leona / Maokai - a known scope boundary, not a new gap. **STALENESS:** **ZERO findings** - the only champion in batch16 with clean ability data, so this verdict carries no data-currency caveat.

- **Maokai** - REFUTE (the Malphite / Dr. Mundo / K'Sante "damage inseparable from durability" class, and its **first HEALTH-coupled instance** - Malphite's coupling is a literal armor ratio, Maokai's is a bonus-HP ratio) - real meta = DDragon PRIMARY **Tank**, **Support 67.1% / Top 18.6% / Jungle 13.1%**, support 52.19% WR Em+ (9,457 games, PR 1.6%, BR 0.18%) vs 51.36% all-ranks over 43,468 (he rewards coordination); top is his worst role (48.45%, D- 106/107). Damage ~**81% magic / 12.6% physical / 6.1% true** (two sources agree to 0.1pp) but only ~13-14% team share with 13.3 assists/game - a utility profile. Signature FIRST legendary = **Locket of the Iron Solari 58.0%** (5,321/9,171, 51.8% WR) then Bandlepipes 23.0% (53.9%); boots **Swiftness 69.0%**; keystone contested (four sources Aftershock for support, one renders Grasp off a page-level aggregate). NEVER builds crit / on-hit / lethality / the Sheen line / AD bruiser - **STRUCTURAL, not preference**: his kit has zero bonus-AD ratios, zero AS scaling, zero armor/MR ratios. Kit: passive heals 4-12.8% max HP with **no AP ratio**; Q 40% AP + 2-4% of **TARGET** max HP (invariant to his own build); W 40% AP; **E 25% AP + 5% CASTER BONUS HP** (brush-enhanced 50% AP + **10% bonus HP**, the only DoT, 3 ticks over 2s); R 75% AP. **No shield, no DR - the "Vengeful Maelstrom 20% DR" that surfaces in search is the pre-2018 ultimate and is NOT live.** DISCRIMINATOR ANSWERED against the Amumu RM-44 / Cho'Gath RM-51 / Galio RM-55 AP-RUSH test: **no separate off-axis AP core is omitted** - an AP legendary opens **2.4%** of games and **LOSES** (Liandry's-first **48.9%**, corroborated **49.2% over 1,852 games**, vs a 52.19% baseline), no AP item appears in any core-slot table, top opens Hollow Radiance and ARAM opens Heartsteel; the one partial exception is **jungle (13.1%, 51.54%, C-) which IS Liandry's-first**, a minority role not the signature. Production tank/ds.ehp leads Randuin's #1 / Warmog's #2 / Heartsteel #3-5 / Kaenic #4 / Jak'Sho #3-5, surfaces Sunfire #11, Hollow Radiance #12, Abyssal #13-14, Iceborn #18, Thornmail #21, and carries **zero AP damage legendaries in the top-40** except Rylai's #37-38 / Bloodletter's #38-39 - CORRECT. SHARPENING in the engine's favour: the ds.ehp HP-heavy lead is **more correct for Maokai than for any other tank in the sweep**, because his E genuinely converts bonus HP into damage. Only imperfection is the SETTLED Alistar / Braum / Leona personal-EHP-vs-team-aura scope limit (Locket #24-25, Bandlepipes #33-35, Knight's Vow #32-36 buried; his first TWO legendaries are aura/utility and his self-EHP bricks are slots 3-5, exactly Leona's shape)
- **Master Yi** - GAP RM-80 (**NEW shape: an on-hit attack-speed carry mis-ROUTED to the bruiser branch**, distinct from the Kalista/Varus on-hit-SERVED REFUTE and from Kindred RM-74) - real meta = JUNGLE mono-role **85.2-89.1%**, all-ranks 49.86% WR (220,357 games) vs Em+ **50.1-50.4% consensus** (one aggregator is a +1.8pp outlier - discard), PR 9.14% falling to ~5.9% at Em+ (low-elo-inflated-pick profile). Damage **65.7% physical / 3.6% magic / 30.6% TRUE** - **true damage rank 2/77 of the whole pool**, sourced from E Wuju Style on-hit plus the Kraken proc chain. Signature FIRST = **Kraken Slayer 76.1%** (40,876/53,681, **50.7% WR**) over BotRK 19.9% (47.7%) and The Collector 3.9%; Kraken is simultaneously most-picked AND highest-WR (+3.0pp), so there is no popularity-vs-performance tension. Core = **Kraken -> Guinsoo's -> Experimental Hexplate** (23.66%, 55.84% WR); boots **Gluttonous Greaves 30.09% at 55.19% vs Berserker's 28.10% at 47.32%** (a 7.9pp gap); both dominant keystones are ATTACK-SPEED keystones and the AS shard is taken in **98.4%** of games. NEVER builds: all AP (no damage ability has an AP ratio), all mana incl. **Essence Reaver**, all lethality, **ALL tank/health items (Randuin's, Warmog's, Jak'Sho, Heartsteel)**, all bruiser-AH items (Black Cleaver, Sundered Sky, Eclipse, Sterak's, Shojin), **Trinity Force notably absent**, and **Terminus absent despite V25.16 explicitly enabling Alpha Strike to stack its Juxtaposition** - a clean natural experiment showing synergy alone is insufficient. Kit = SUSTAINED on-hit AS DPS: Q applies on-hit at **75% effectiveness** and **refunds 1s CD per auto**, E is TRUE damage on-hit (basic attacks only), R grants +25/45/65% AS; AS is **superlinear** for him (raises DPS + Q uptime + proc frequency at once). DISCRIMINATOR ANSWERED: **ON-HIT decisively, slot-1 split 96.1% on-hit / 3.9% crit, Infinity Edge as first item = 0%** - and this is mechanically real, not a labelling accident, because **Kraken carries no crit chance** and **Guinsoo's crit-to-on-hit conversion no longer exists**; crit is a slot-3-to-5 amp layer and even the "crit path" opens on-hit. Production bruiser/ds.hybrid (his DEFAULT route) leads **BotRK #1** (his LOSING 19.9% opener) over signature Kraken #2-3, and the bruiser route **imports an entire class of never-built items** - Trinity #2-3, Heartsteel #4-6, Essence Reaver #4-7, Eclipse #4-9, Stormrazor #5-6, Yun Tal #7-10, Terminus #8-15, Sundered Sky, Hullbreaker, Shieldbow, Sterak's - while burying his real 2nd core **Guinsoo's #9-12** and his real 3rd **Experimental Hexplate #31-36 (DEEP)**. Hexplate is structurally unrankable by damage sim (its value is ULT haste + a post-ultimate AS burst timed to Highlander), the same mechanism as Lissandra's Malignance. Neither reroute helps: carry/ds.dps is only marginally better (BotRK #1, Kraken #2, Trinity #3) and ds.onhit is WORSE (BotRK #1, **Liandry's #2**). Fix = route him off bruiser to the on-hit/carry family + credit AS superlinearity and ult haste. Note **slot-4/5/6 win rates in his tables are survivorship-biased by game length** (Kraken 50.6% climbing to Shieldbow 63.9%) - only slot-1 comparisons are clean
- **Mel** - GAP RM-47 SHARED (the Aurora / Lux Luden's-buried burst-mage twin) **PLUS the discovery champion for RM-81 (NEW): verified corpus-wide ability-data STALENESS** - PREMISE CORRECTION: she did **not** release in 2026; she released **2025-01-23** and was **REWORKED in patch 26.03**, champion key **800**, with 117,831 all-ranks games, so she is NOT an RM-79 coverage case and `champion_has_ability_data` returns True. Real meta = **BOTTOM 56.1% / mid 24.8% / support 18.6%** (a bot-lane AP carry, not a mid mage), WR 47.04% all-ranks / 47.35% Em+ bot, and a **31-36% ban rate on a sub-48% champion** - banned like a tyrant, performs bottom-tier. Damage **93.0% magic / 6.0% physical / 1.0% true**, the physical being basic attacks only since she has no physical-damage ability. Signature FIRST = **Luden's Echo, 5 of 5 aggregators, 80.89% pick** (45.71% WR, 21,626 games) -> Shadowflame 72.81% -> **Rocketbelt 52.2%** -> Rabadon's 40.66% -> Zhonya's 35.57%; Sorcerer's 84.72%, Arcane Comet 86.3%. **Kit is FRONT-LOADED BURST with ZERO %maxHP and ZERO %missing-HP scaling anywhere** - the passive execute stores FLAT damage vs current health + shields, Q's first explosion is **57.8% of base and 55% of the AP ratio and the whole ability resolves in 0.5s**, R is a single instantaneous global nuke, and the only DoT (E's field) is the smallest source in the kit and gated behind a 1.5s root. AH is LOW-MODERATE and **actively rejected** (the haste boot is 12.1% at a WORSE 44.35% WR than Sorcerer's 45.93%; Malignance does not appear in her table at all). Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's Torment #1 - the LOWEST-win-rate item on her entire table (40.24% WR, 3.43% pick), losing the first-item contest ~23.6 to 1** - plus Blackfire #2, Rabadon's #3, Void #4, and **buries her 80.89%-pick signature Luden's at #11-13** with Rocketbelt #27-28 (the settled can't-value-active-engage limit, cf. Kennen RM-73). SHARED with Aurora/Lux because scorer, defect, buried signature and fix are identical; severity HIGH within the family since she has **no %maxHP coefficient at all**, so Liandry's synergizes with literally nothing. **She also breaks the sweep's standing "Luden's appears ZERO times" systemic claim** - and the engine here disagrees with **RC's OWN scraped `data/meta_build/sr_champion_builds.json`**, which already lists Luden's > Shadowflame > Rabadon's. Selection-bias trap for any win-rate lever: **Mejai's shows 73.11% WR at 6.05% pick**. **RM-81, independently re-probed and CONFIRMED** against `data/daemon_slayer/16.14.1/champion_abilities.json`: Q per-explosion base **13/15.5/18/20.5/23** (live 5/7/9/11/13), W cooldown **35/32/29/26/23** (live 38/35/32/29/26), W `effects_descriptions` still reads **"becoming invulnerable to non-turret damage"** (a mechanic REMOVED in 26.03 and replaced by a shield), R base **100/150/200** (live 125/200/275); the file header carries **`meraki_content_patch: 25.15` despite `fetched_at: 2026-07-16`**, and **Maokai independently corroborates** (Q base 65/110/155/200/245 = the exact pre-V26.03 "from" values). So the RM-79 blocker is broader than missing champions - it also means **stale VALUES for all 171 champions that ARE present**. RM-81 is a DISTINCT spec from RM-79 because the mechanism is **staleness, not absence**: the RM-79 kit-less guard still reports healthy (`champion_has_ability_data` returns True). **CORRECTION to this finding's first framing:** the freeze itself is NOT undetected - `tools/daemon_slayer_abilities_extract.py:128` deliberately PINS `_EXPECTED_MERAKI_CONTENT_PATCH = "25.15"` and logs the frozen-`latest` caveat explicitly (it warns only if Meraki moves PAST the pin), and `tools/upstream_drift_check.py` already tracks the field. What is genuinely NEW is the never-quantified DOWNSTREAM cost: the pin is ~11 patches old, so every champion REWORKED since 25.15 is mis-modelled at the per-champion valuation layer and **a coverage scan keyed on missing champions cannot catch it**. Mel is the worst instance found because DS models her W as invulnerability and her Q as uniform-per-explosion, and the front-load coefficient is precisely the quantity that decides the burst-vs-DoT item question this sweep keeps adjudicating; reworked champions are the highest-risk cohort. Minor same-family drift: `sr_champion_builds.json` `kit_notes` says "Projectile reflection (R)" when the reflect is on **W**, and lists her role as Mid
- **Milio** - REFUTE (the Janna / Ivern / Lulu / Sona Support-primary enchanter class - 9 of 10 enchanters, only Karma inverts) - real meta = DDragon **`tags = ["Support","Mage"]`, primary tag SUPPORT** (verified locally, key 902), **SUPPORT 99.6%** of games (mid 0.2% is ~190 games of off-role fill; the "Milio Mid"/"Milio ADC" aggregator pages are generated templates, one stale at patch 16.11). All-ranks 51.42% WR / 3.98% PR (96,670 games, S- rank 9/93); Em+ 50.62-52.14%. **THE ROUTING TRAP stated explicitly: a classifier keying on `tags[0]` gets Support and routes correctly, but a membership test like `if "Mage" in tags` fires on the SECONDARY tag and misroutes him onto an AP-damage scorer** - RC routes correctly (`default_for_champion` -> `(enchanter, mage)`, live probe lands on ds.hps). Damage ~81% magic but only ~6.1k/game, and the one source publishing a percentile places him near the **0.6th percentile, lowest among all supports** - the low share IS the finding. **MODELING TRAP CONFIRMED AND RESOLVED against local DDragon: Dream Maker (3870) is a 400g-total / 0g-base depth-2 SUPPORT-QUEST upgrade, NOT a legendary** (siblings Celestial Opposition 3869, Solstice Sleigh 3876, Zaz'Zak's 3871, Bloodsong 3877) - the SAME trap logged at Lulu, now confirmed cohort-wide rather than a one-off. Signature = Dream Maker 90.85% (not a legendary) -> **FIRST LEGENDARY Echoes of Helia (2200g) 57% pick / 50.9% WR**, heading 100% of the top-3 core combinations -> Ionian Boots 94.32% -> Moonstone 48% -> Ardent Censer -> Mikael's/Redemption; Summon Aery 90.9%. **He builds NO offensive AP damage core: Rabadon's, Liandry's and Blackfire ALL fall below the display threshold across 96,670 games** - absent, not low-pick - and Sorcerer's Shoes is 0.17% at a losing 46.51%. Kit: Q 120% AP, W heals 15% AP + grants **10-20% of ally BASE ATTACK RANGE**, E shields 45% AP on **2 charges**, R heals 50% AP + **cleanses all non-airborne CC + 65% tenacity**; nominal split is ~55/45 damage-to-utility but realized allocation is far more lopsided because **every remaining effect (range grant, move speed, cleanse, tenacity) has ZERO AP scaling** - so roughly half his kit value is flat rank-scaled utility AP never touches, and any pure AP-ratio-sum valuation undervalues him.
