Production enchanter/ds.hps leads **Echoes of Helia #1 - which IS his ground-truth first legendary**, so the bright line passes cleanly; **Milio is the FIRST enchanter in the sweep where the Helia lead is CORRECT** (at Lulu the real #1 was Ardent and the lead was one slot off). Two imperfections, both settled class-level scope limits: his real 2nd core **Moonstone ranks DEAD LAST #9** of the 9-item pool, and Redemption/Shurelya's/Dawncore are thin or absent. **NEW SYSTEMIC EVIDENCE that sharpens the Lulu finding from "near-flat" to "proven INVARIANT": the batch15 probe ran the enchanter reroute on all five champions - a TANK (Maokai), an AD on-hit skirmisher (Master Yi), two mages and the actual enchanter - and the ds.hps output is BYTE-IDENTICAL in all five, same 9 items, same order.** The ranking is therefore driven entirely by each item's own heal-shield power and not at all by the champion's kit, so Milio's correct #1 is correct *by coincidence of Helia's statline*. REFUTE stands on the bright line, but the mechanism underneath is hollow - a cohort-level lift, not a per-champ RM. Also: highest-pick and highest-WR first legendary DIVERGE (Helia 57%/50.9% vs **Ardent Censer 58.0% WR at only 7.7% pick**), so a raw-WR ranker picks the wrong opener
- **Mordekaiser** - GAP RM-82 (**NEW** - an AP juggernaut whose signature Rylai's opener and Riftmaker 2nd are buried under a never-built pure-AP-amp + magic-pen lead) - real meta = **TOP 89.2%** mono-role, Em+ consensus 52.3-54.6% WR / ~8% PR, S/S+ and top-10 by every source; all-ranks 53.98% WR / 10.59% PR / **14.03% BR** over 256,952 games (a low-elo terror respected less at high elo - pro play is only 50.4% over 1,371 picks). Damage ~**82.8% magic / 12.35% physical / 4.84% true**, plus **healing 11,520/match (rank 25/107)**. Signature FIRST = **Rylai's Crystal Scepter 62.73% of all first-item buys (54.69% WR)**, a 2.7x margin over the runner-up, with all five most-picked full paths opening Rylai's; SECOND = **Riftmaker 44.83% (56.08%)**; **Liandry's is only 4.12% of first buys** (13.81% at slot 2). Most-picked complete path Rylai's -> Riftmaker -> Liandry's at 15.18% / 57.59% / 3,997 games; Steelcaps 63.11%, Conqueror 83.78%. NEVER builds crit, lethality, **mana (STRUCTURAL - he is MANALESS, no resource bar, so mana is a literally dead stat and safe to hard-code)**, AP burst (**Rabadon's only 0.43% / 163 games on an AP champion** - decisive evidence his scaling is SUSTAIN not burst; Shadowflame 0.26%; Luden's/Stormsurge/Horizon/Malignance absent), on-hit, or enchanter; **he builds NO pure magic-penetration item at any slot** (Void Staff absent from its own 57-champion roster, Cryptbloom absent from its 49-champion roster). IMPORTANT EXCEPTION - **do NOT blanket-exclude tank**: Thornmail 19.46% (7,349 games) and Randuin's 6.42% are live; only Sunfire, Warmog's and Hollow Radiance are true zeros. Kit: passive **Darkness Rise is a genuine SUSTAINED DoT** - 5 (+30% AP) (+**1-5% of target MAXIMUM health**) per second as **8 ticks/sec at 0.125s**, so **he already owns a %maxHP burn natively before buying one**; **W Indestructible carries NO AP ratio at all** and is capped at **30% of his MAXIMUM HEALTH**, converting 35-45% of the remainder to healing - its entire budget scales off max health and damage throughput; **E grants 5-15% magic penetration natively**; R steals 13% of target stats. AH is concentrated on W (12->8s) and R (140->100s), never Q (already 4s) - why Hexplate 17.87% and Riftmaker/Spirit Visage are core while zero-AH burst items are absent. Production mage/ds.ability leads Liandry's #1 / Blackfire #2 / **Rabadon's #3** / **Void Staff #4** / Shadowflame #4-5 / Mejai's #5-6 / Stormsurge #6-7 / **Cryptbloom #8-11** / **Riftmaker #8-10**, and buries his signature **Rylai's at #25**. GAP not REFUTE despite the sustained-DoT class being right, because the bright line fails (engine #1 = 4.12% of his first buys) and **FOUR of the engine's top-8 are items he provably never builds**. THREE unmodeled axes: (1) **damage DELIVERED not dealt** - Rylai's slow keeps targets inside an 8-tick/sec proximity aura and a damage sim prices a slow at ~zero; **this is the SAME item at the SAME rank (#25) by the SAME mechanism already logged as a secondary under-rank at Malzahar**, promoted here from footnote to headline because for Mordekaiser it is the FIRST item; (2) **survivability-coupled AP** - W has no AP ratio and scales on max health, so Riftmaker's health-AP-omnivamp package beats raw AP amp and a pure-AP scorer is wrong by ~170x in pick share; (3) **native-penetration saturation** - his E already grants 5-15% pen with diminishing multiplicative return and pen items grant zero survivability. DISTINCT from the Lillia/Brand/Cassiopeia sustained-DoT REFUTEs (their engine #1 Liandry's IS their real first), from the Ahri RM-40 burst family (he is not front-loaded), and from RM-63 (Blackfire is not his item). Traps: Liandry's-first lines post HIGHER win rates (60.86% on 764 games) but are low-n selection bias - do not chase them over the 62.73% first-slot consensus; one aggregator's Steelcaps -> Riftmaker -> Rylai's display is highest-WR, not most-picked; a keystone mis-parse returned "Press the Attack 47,332 games" which is the aggregate section row (98.5% of sample), **Conqueror is the keystone** (same artifact class as the Maokai Grasp mis-parse this batch); and the 16.14 notes carry Liandry's/Blackfire/Conqueror lines under an **Azir-specific** bugfix heading that are NOT global buffs. Patch context: **16.14 BUFFED him** (E 60/75/90/105/120 +40% AP -> 60/80/100/120/140 +45% AP; R stat steal 10% -> 13%), reading as delayed compensation for a 16.10 Riftmaker bugfix that hit his core 2nd item
- **Locke** - GAP RM-79 (**NEW SHAPE and the most severe mis-valuation in the sweep to date** - not a scorer-valuation gap but a DATA-COVERAGE gap that silently degrades to a wrong-axis scorer) - real meta = AP burst assassin, MID ~64-67% (jungle ~22% at a weak 45.29% WR), released 2026-06-24 patch 26.13 as the 173rd champion, nerfed in 26.14; WR 50.38% all-ranks (315,888 games) to 53.58% Em+ mid (46,233), PR ~7.9%, **BR 55-74%**. All magic except R Purgatory's execute which deals RAW/TRUE damage (so magic pen is worth LESS on the execute). **FRONT-LOADED BURST - no enemy-facing DoT anywhere**; the only over-time element is W's SELF-damage and self-heal. Signature FIRST = **Lich Bane (53.63% WR over 44,152 games)** via Doran's Ring -> Sheen -> Lich Bane -> Sorcerer's -> Shadowflame -> Zhonya's/Rabadon's; Electrocute 93.4%. NEVER builds crit, **on-hit (Nashor's absent DESPITE an on-hit passive)**, lethality/AD, **Liandry's (completely absent across four aggregators)**, mana-stack, Riftmaker. THE DEFECT, root-caused live: `champion_abilities.json` has **171 keys vs champions.json 173** - the missing two are **Locke (805)** and **Zaahen (904)**, both also absent from cdragon_ability_ratios / cdragon_spell_stats / wiki_ability_stats / wiki_stats. With no ability data, ds.ability and ds.burst return all-zero deltas, tripping the documented `_kitless_all_zero` fail-soft and falling through to **ds.dps**. Controlled probe: **Lux (has data) mage -> `ability`, assassin -> `burst`; Locke -> `dps`/`dps`; Zaahen -> `dps`/`dps`.** Note `default_for_champion("Locke")` correctly returns (mage, assassin) - the ROUTING is right, the SCORER degrades underneath it. Result: all three of his routes return byte-identical ds.dps rankings led by **BotRK #1-2 / Heartsteel #1-2 / Trinity #3-6 / Essence Reaver #4-7 / Stormrazor #5 / Voltaic #6-8**, with never-built Liandry's #3-8, his signature **Lich Bane only #13-16**, and his ENTIRE AP core (Rabadon's / Shadowflame / Void Staff / Zhonya's / Luden's / Malignance) **ABSENT from the top-40** - a categorically wrong AXIS, not a mis-ordering, and unrepairable by any valuation lever because the kit is simply not in the data. **CORRECTION to the original research read:** his `attackdamageperlevel = 0` is NOT a Riot anti-AD-build lock - DDragon ships 0 for EVERY champion (Riot stopped exporting AD growth) and the real value is Meraki-backfilled; exactly 4 champions stored 0 (Locke, Zaahen, Yunara, Senna) and only **Senna's is genuine**. FIX STATUS: (a) ability data is **BLOCKED UPSTREAM** - Meraki's bulk map carries exactly 171 champions and its per-champion URLs 404 for both, so re-running cannot fix it (CommunityDragon HAS both at HTTP 200, but lacks Meraki's typed `damage_blocks`, so a CDragon fallback is a new parser and an operator decision); (b) the ds.hybrid kitless guard **SHIPPED** - a row-delta check cannot catch it since auto-DPS + EHP stay non-zero, so the bruiser branch now flags it champion-side via `champion_has_ability_data()`, which matters most for Zaahen whose DEFAULT route IS bruiser; (c) the degraded-mode signal was **already implemented** for ability/burst/hps (`Locke mage -> fell_back=True, archetype relabelled "carry"`) - only hybrid was silent; (d) a SECOND defect found and **SHIPPED**: the perlevel backfill read Meraki's per-champion endpoint (404s for new champs) instead of the bulk map, silently persisting false zeros - now bulk-first, recovering **Yunara's real 2.5** (she is a marksman) and taking the roster from 169 to 170 filled

AP is shield/heal power: E shield +50% AP, R ally max-health +55% AP, W move speed +5% per 100 AP. Signature FIRST LEGENDARY = **Ardent Censer** (55.34% pick / 51.64% WR / 23,624 games) via World Atlas -> Ionian Boots (84-93%) -> Ardent -> Dream Maker -> Moonstone -> Shurelya's/Mikael's. **MODELING TRAP: Dream Maker is NOT a legendary** (item 3870, 400g, depth 2, off the World Atlas line, tagged GoldPer/Lane/Vision) yet two sites call it her "first item" - treating it as one would distort any build model. Two CROSS-ENCHANTER calibration sub-findings (cohort-wide, NOT Lulu gaps): the engine leads Echoes of Helia while the field's #1 is Ardent Censer (real Helia pick on Lulu is 0.379%) - right class, one slot off; and item deltas are near-FLAT across champions (Helia 27.33 Lulu / 27.15 Janna / 27.45 Soraka / 27.43 Sona) even though baselines correctly differentiate (Lulu 3.263 shield-only vs Soraka 9.811 heal-dominant), i.e. the ranking is driven by the item's own heal-shield power, not the champion's kit shape. Q and R carry no armor ratio but R is a 100-130s ultimate, so everything he casts repeatedly is armor-scaled. Signature FIRST = **Sunfire Aegis (57.66% WR / 1,455 games)** -> Plated Steelcaps -> Frozen Heart / Thornmail / Jak'Sho / Kaenic / Randuin's; Arcane Comet 69.7%; Iceborn appears in NO source's core table. TWO BUILDS RESOLVED: the AP variant is **fringe AND strictly losing** - ~5-12% share at **46.9-49.1% WR against a tank band of 50.65-53.9%** (AP jungle 47.4%, AP support 45.3%), with four of seven sources surfacing zero AP items; it DOES rush AP first (Malignance), the one GAP condition it satisfies, but a fringe LOSING build is not an omitted meta core. **Framing correction: the current AP core is Malignance -> Stormsurge -> Shadowflame, NOT Rocketbelt/Nashor's/Liandry's/Blackfire** (stale from earlier seasons - do not seed those). Only imperfection: signature-FIRST Sunfire under-ranked #11 - the class-level EHP-scorer scope limit already settled at K'Sante (Iceborn #18) and Braum. **RESIDUAL CHECK RUN AND CLOSED:** the research flagged that if the engine did NOT encode his armor ratios he would be mis-valued for a different reason (a scorer-INPUT defect). Checked `champion_abilities.json` directly - **the ratios ARE fully encoded**: E `base [70..230], ap_pct 60, caster_armor_pct 40`; W active `ap_pct 20, caster_armor_pct 15`; W aftershock `ap_pct 30, caster_armor_pct 15`; plus the W passive armor grant. GOTCHA: damage blocks store scaling under `base`/`ap_pct`/`caster_armor_pct`, **NOT** `raw_modifiers` - a raw_modifiers-only dump makes them look empty and would have produced a false "ratios missing" finding

Every RM-63 member applies AoE or spreading burn (Heimerdinger turrets, Hwei's book, Karthus E Defile), so **the fix is item-side: make the Blackfire AP grant target-count-aware**, not a per-champ override. Secondary under-ranks: Rylai's #25 despite 67% pick ("damage DELIVERED not dealt" - it keeps targets inside Null Zone) and Zhonya's despite the highest high-sample WR (its value is CHANNEL COMPLETION on a 2.5s suppression, zero simulated damage); over-rank in the other direction: **Riftmaker #8-10 with literally zero meta presence** (his passive Void Shift, a periodic 90% DR + CC immunity, is what makes early defensive/hybrid items redundant). **COHORT-WIDE TRAP: Mejai's ranks #5-6 in EVERY ds.ability output observed this sweep**, yet on Malzahar it is 1.13% pick with an 83.72% WR over 301 games - textbook snowball selection bias that any win-rate-trained lever must exclude

Steep decay, now SOURCED: WR by game length 53.49% @10min -> 50.28 @20 -> 48.29 @25 -> 47.01 @35 -> 41.57 @50, **delta -6.48pp (10->35min)**, crossing below his own average at ~24-25min - items should be priced on early tempo. Recent patches: 26.10 rework (E 100% -> 90%), 26.12 nerfs (AD growth 3.7 -> 3.4, Q 95% -> 90%); 26.13/26.14 no changes. Meta plays him as a skirmisher/bruiser NOT a burst assassin (Conqueror ~91.6% vs Electrocute 0.90%). Production bruiser/ds.hybrid over-leads **never-built on-hit BotRK #1 + DEAD-signature Trinity #2 + off-meta Essence Reaver #3**, surfaces his signature **Eclipse only #4 (tanky L16) / #8 (squishy L16)**, buries 2nd-core **Sundered Sky #17-19**, Black Cleaver #29-40, Sterak's #32-33, Death's Dance #34-40 (Guardian Angel absent), with heavy never-built crit pollution (Stormrazor #5, LDR #6, Kraken #7, IE #8, Yun Tal #15, Collector #22, Hexoptics #23); no reroute helps. NEW ROOT CAUSE distinct from the family: passive **Flurry grants +40% AS on only the next 2 autos after an ability cast** - a bounded burst window, not uptime (and Q2/W do NOT apply on-hit during the dash) - and the auto-DPS sub-model **extrapolates it into steady-state attack speed**, floating on-hit/crit/AS to the top. So this is an OVER-CREDIT of a bounded proc where Aatrox/Hecarim/Jarvan are an UNDER-CREDIT of an unmodeled ability-haste axis: opposite mechanisms, same symptom. Compounding: energy (not mana, 200 flat) makes cooldowns the real constraint so AH is underpriced; E Tempest deals MAGIC damage off a +90% BONUS AD ratio (a type/scaling mismatch). NOT a data gap - Eclipse (6692) and Sundered Sky (6610) are both in the 16.14.1 pool with modeled effects and the auxiliary axes are wired; the item ranker just does not consume them. Severity MILD-to-MODERATE (Eclipse IS surfaced #4, unlike Aatrox/Hecarim whose signature was absent or >45); closest sibling is Jarvan RM-65

Signature FIRST = **Malignance** (~59% first-item share, 53%+ WR) -> Sorcerer's -> Shadowflame -> Zhonya's -> Rabadon's -> Void Staff. **Her kit contains ZERO damage-over-time** - every source is a single instant instance (Q 75% / W 70% / E 60% / R 75% AP), and the one persistent zone is wiki-hard-capped to one damage instance per cast (the 0.25s refresh is the SLOW only); full combo E-W-R-Q = **280% AP inside ~1.5s**; **no %maxHP scaling anywhere**, so a %maxHP-burn item gets no kit synergy multiplier. **Liandry's is NOT core**: slot-6 18% / slot-5 9% buy at **49% WR, BELOW her 50.8% baseline**, absent from six other aggregators and from every pro build (the slot-6 56% WR is a survivorship artifact). Production mage/ds.ability leads **Liandry's #1** + Blackfire #2, Rabadon's #3, Shadowflame #4-5, Zhonya's #10-12, **Malignance #15**, Rocketbelt #28. SHARED with Ahri rather than a new RM because the scorer, the defect (a %maxHP-DoT lead imposed on a front-loaded burst profile), the **buried signature item AND rank (Malignance #15)** and the fix are all identical - the Karthus->Heimerdinger/Hwei RM-63 precedent; the rest of the burst-mage family took their own RMs only because each buried a DIFFERENT signature (Annie RM-45, Aurora RM-47, Elise RM-54, Gragas RM-59, Kennen RM-73). EXTRA SEVERITY: the displaced item's value is concentrated in **+20 ULTIMATE ability haste** (~80s -> ~53s on her rank-3 R, swinging both engage and panic button), which a pure-damage simulator prices at ~zero - so the right item is **structurally unrankable by damage simulation alone**, not merely mis-ordered. Second under-credit: **Zhonya's is a SECOND STASIS, not a stat stick** (she is one of very few champs with two stasis buttons), and her R self-cast (2.5s stasis + a heal scaling 1:1 with missing HP) is an EHP-numerator effect on the ULTIMATE no item scorer sees. Only source disagreement is a pro-vs-solo split (pros favor Blackfire / Rocketbelt) - neither of which is Liandry's either, so it does not threaten the verdict

The only nuance = her energized-AS signature Statikk Shiv under-ranked #13 (the carry/on-hit scorer under-models energized items), an item-specific scope limit NOT an axis gap. Her DEFAULT route (carry) SERVES her, UNLIKE Bel'Veth RM-49 whose default bruiser route buried on-hit -> REFUTE not GAP

This is UNLIKE Janna/Ivern (DDragon PRIMARY = Support -> enchanter default, SERVED -> REFUTE): Karma's Mage-primary tag routes her wrong by default. Fix = role-aware default (route support-Karma to enchanter), the Galio RM-55 inverted twin (Galio's default tank mis-served his dominant mid-mage; Karma's default mage mis-serves her dominant support-enchanter) + the Bel'Veth reroute-fix shape. NOT a mage-scorer over-lead GAP (her dominant build is enchanter, not poke)

Do NOT re-file it as "model the passive" or as "the model is still incomplete". (mild, closest-to-REFUTE) - crit-marksman/on-hit-hybrid on the correct carry/ds.dps, but leads not-built on-hit (BotRK #1, Runaan's #2) over his crit primary + buries his #2 core Hexoptics C44 #14; his real #1 item Kraken IS surfaced (#3); root cause = reworked Dirty Fighting 200%-crit double-shot unmodeled; same family as Lucian RM-37 (batch)
- **Akali** - GAP RM-41 (CLASS-LEVEL) - pure-AP burst assassin; production ds.burst serves her damage core but leaks AD-spellblade pollution (Trinity #3, Essence Reaver #4, BotRK #9, IE #12) into a 100%-AP champ + buries Hextech Gunblade #11; hits the whole _AP_ASSASSIN_IDS cohort except Leblanc (Fizz/Katarina Trinity #2); first ds.burst-scorer gap, mirror of RM-35
- **Aatrox** - GAP RM-39 - ability-centric AD drain-bruiser mis-served by the ds.hybrid auto-DPS sub-model; leads Blade of the Ruined King on-hit #1 + crit ADC items, buries his #1 item Spear of Shojin at rank 51/143; NOT pool/burst/coherence (new shape, first bruiser-scorer gap)
- **Ahri** - GAP RM-40 (mildest) - burst-combo mage on the correct mage/ds.ability scorer, but the sustained DPS model leads Liandry's #1 (off-meta vs squishy) + buries her universal signature Malignance at #15 (ult-haste->burst unmodeled); most AP core surfaces top-10; NOT pool/wrong-archetype; first mage-scorer gap
- **Corki** - GAP RM-38 - AD-caster spell-weaver; Trinity/Shojin pool-filtered + no AD-caster model; 14.10 rework = true-per-auto + Sheen-special-cased **MODEL HALF SHIPPED 2026-08-04 (ENGINE 1.272.0, DEFAULT-OFF `apply_ad_axis_ability_damage` on the CARRY ranker).** He carries two PHYSICAL per-spell rows at `ap_pct_sum` 0.0 (dps 2.738 + 5.551 at L16 vs the tanky target) so the term is nonzero for him and his order REORDERS, live-verified over `/rank`. POOL half was already shipped and needs BOTH seams: `exempt_offclass_by_win` carries his Trinity Force, `widen_carry_pool` carries Shojin, and they COMPOSE (pool 109 / 112 / 113).
- **Ezreal** - GAP RM-36 - AD-caster spell-weaver mis-routed to sustained auto-DPS; Trinity/Shojin pool-filtered **STILL OPEN 2026-08-04 and the reason is now MEASURED - do NOT re-file this as 'port the AD-axis term to the carry scorer', that shipped at ENGINE 1.272.0 and is a provable NO-OP for him.** His only PHYSICAL per-spell row is Q Mystic Shot (dps 18.447 at L16 vs the tanky target) and its `ap_pct_sum` is 200.0, so the term's AP-SCALING EXCLUSION - the deliberate RM-39 contract that keeps an AD-axis term from becoming an AP-pricing channel - drops it and his credited sum is exactly 0.0. Closing RM-36 needs a SPLIT credit for the AD PORTION of a dual-scaling row (the honest analogue of the held MIXED 50 pct treatment), NOT a widen of that gate. The POOL half is shipped, both seams, and they compose: Ezreal at both flags is pool 113 with Trinity Force #4 and Spear of Shojin #42.
- **Jhin** - REFUTE - AS-locked lethality-crit; already mapped fight_length=0.5, correctly served
- **Kai'Sa** - REFUTE - poke/Manamune not primary; on-hit hybrid correctly served
- **Lucian** - GAP RM-37 - crit-sustained correctly routed but leads off-meta on-hit + Essence Reaver coherence-docked
- **Miss Fortune** - GAP RM-35 - lethality-crit burst mis-valued as on-hit; needs L3 crit-burst table + AP-exclusion
- **Qiyana** - FENCED RM-34 - AD-assassin pure-lethality DATA-REFUTED
- **Talon** - FENCED RM-34 - AD-assassin pure-lethality DATA-REFUTED
- **Varus** - REFUTE - on-hit W-max IS his primary; engine on-hit lead MATCHES the meta
- **Zed** - FENCED RM-34 - AD-assassin pure-lethality DATA-REFUTED (R114/R115)
